## Retired at the 2026-10-25 landing (roster-r12) — operational docs, spent

*Provenance: `roster-redesign-brief.md` was the live rider for the full-roster
redesign + sigil commission; the commission ran to completion through the
artist-kit route (its kit + prompt superseded the rider's paste-the-canonical
mechanics, but the rider's doctrine — sanctioned zones, sigil map, ghost rule,
invariants — is what the kit headers and prompt carry forward).
`reconcile-runbook.md` was the 12-step landing checklist; the landing executed
2026-10-25 across phases 1–3 (promotion chain recorded in GAMES.md Current
state and `scratch/paste-ledger.md`). Both are retired VERBATIM per their own
lifecycle rules; neither governs anything anymore. Future commissions rebuild
fresh riders/kits against current canonical — that is the rider doctrine.*

### roster-redesign-brief.md (verbatim)

# ROSTER REDESIGN + SIGILS — weapon artist brief (full-roster commission)

You are the weapon artist for **SPINCLASH**, a physics auto-battler preview. Your mission has two halves, in this order:

- **A. SIGILS for all 22 weapons** — give every weapon its carrier mark: exactly ONE diegetic element rendered in the wielding fighter's cast color (the mechanism is pre-wired — you tag the shape, the engine colors it per carrier, live in the preview).
- **B. REDESIGNS as the owner directs, in-session** — he is reviewing the roster and will name the weapons he doesn't like, with direction. Treat his in-session words as the spec and iterate with him against the live preview (this is how the scroll and the orb found their looks — those sessions are the standard).

You deliver **the complete updated file** (`spinclash-preview.html`, pasted into this session with this brief). Scope: **art + art-coupled data only — ZERO sim lines, ZERO renderer lines, ZERO CFG lines.** The sigil mechanism already exists in your base; you use it, you never touch it.

---

## OUTPUT RULES

1. **Return the complete file** — never a diff, fragment, patch or summary.
2. **Never generate, request or reference images.** All art is polygon data through `build()`, spine-local.
3. **Never ask questions in the delivery.** In-session, the owner IS the question loop — iterate with him. Judgment calls that survive to the final file get one-line `observed: …` notes in each weapon's header comment.
4. **Stay inside the SANCTIONED ZONES** (below). Everything else byte-identical.

## BASE FILE TRUTH

The pasted canonical outranks this brief, its comments, and any memory: **code beats comments.** STALE-BASE markers — current iff ALL hold: (1) the header/title stamp reads **`build thrash-r11`**; (2) `CFG` contains `flipMs`; (3) `const WEAPON_IDS` ends `'orb'];`; (4) `const KC = '@carrier'` exists beside the palette consts; (5) `CFG` contains `reArmDeep`. Any failure: deliver anyway, begin every touched header with `STALE BASE:`.

Ghost rule stands: `CIRCLES_V2[id] || []` self-heals; weapons with no row boot as non-contacting ghosts under the `ART PREVIEW — HITBOXES UNBAKED` watermark. **You never author covering circles.**

## THE SIGIL MECHANISM (pre-wired — read, then use)

Beside the palette consts lives `const KC = '@carrier'`. A shape tagged `[KC, points]` (or `[KC, points, 1]` for paint-only) renders **in the wielding fighter's cast color** — the swap lives in `drawWeapon`/`drawWeaponChain`, with a gold fallback for carrier-less renders. In the live preview you and the owner will see each weapon's sigil in Speedy's gold on one bot and Heavy's coral on the other, swapping with the dice each round. `KC` is an invalid CSS color on purpose: if a sigil ever renders unswapped it fails visibly instead of silently.

## THE SIGIL DOCTRINE (owner decision 2026-10-23, his words)

*"i dont want it to be like all weapons have the same dot or similar, i want it to make sense for the weapons … on swords there can be dents or something similar … it makes sense to change these or similar patterns, dont just invent something on the sword just for the sake of givinig it a color, like put a string or something stupid on the sword, which doesnt make sense."*

The rules, distilled:

1. **Diegetic only.** The sigil is an element the weapon could genuinely have — an inlay, a wrap, a seal, a hub, a ferrule, a maker's mark, a heraldic device, a dyed string on a *bow* (strings are stupid on swords and correct on bows). Never a sticker.
2. **Exactly one per weapon.** One element wears the carrier color; every other band, gem and fitting keeps its palette color. One glance, one answer to "whose is it."
3. **Recolor-first.** If the weapon already carries a plausible ownership element (a gold band, a gem, a hub, a binding), the sigil RECOLORS it — change that shape's color const to `KC` and nothing else. This is zero-geometry: the silhouette union is untouched, so that weapon's `CIRCLES_V2` row stays valid and must NOT be deleted.
4. **Add only where none exists** — the smallest plausible element, **interior to the silhouette** wherever possible (interior paint = union unchanged = row stays valid). An added shape that changes the silhouette makes the weapon a ghost (row deletion, below).
5. **Never on KP.** The near-white cutting/striking faces mean "this hits" — sigils sit clear of them.
6. **Owner-reference colors are not sigil material:** the scroll's KR ribbon stays the owner's red; the tome's KP pages stay paper.

## THE MARK MAP (starting proposals — the owner's in-session word overrides any line)

| Weapon | Sigil | Class |
|---|---|---|
| Hammer | inlaid stripe on one head face | add (interior) |
| Dagger | maker's mark/notch on the blade flat near the guard — NOT a band near the body (the owner rejected those historically) | add (interior) |
| Axe | socket band in carrier (the lashing that binds head to haft) | add (interior) |
| Sword | maker's mark on the flat near the guard | add (interior) |
| Spear | socket lashing | add (interior) |
| Mace | boss-centre inlay | add (interior) |
| Shield | the existing gold star device — heraldry is what shields carry | recolor |
| Staff | the tip gem (KB → carrier) | recolor |
| Wand | the gem (KB → carrier) | recolor |
| Bow | the string (dyed cord) or a riser inlay — artist/owner pick | recolor/add |
| Crossbow | an existing fitting if present, else a stock inlay | recolor/add |
| Boomerang | one painted wing band | add (interior) |
| Shuriken | the centre hub | recolor |
| Flail | a handle band | recolor |
| Nunchaku | the stick band (the gold one at d 58–64) | recolor |
| Scythe | the ferrule | recolor |
| Katana | the tsuba (the octagonal guard) — and/or the tsuka wrap if the owner prefers | recolor |
| Knives | the binding bar | recolor |
| Javelin | the grip binding (or the socket collar — one, not both) | recolor |
| Tome | the strap (the gem stays KB) | recolor |
| Scroll | a wax seal on the ribbon (small, interior to the barrel) — the ribbon stays KR, the caps stay KG | add (interior) |
| Magic Orb | the rune band | recolor |

Most of the roster is recolor-class: zero geometry, rows intact, gate unchanged. That is the intended shape of this pass.

## REDESIGNS (half B)

The owner names them in-session; until he does, redesign nothing. When he does:

- His word is the spec; iterate against the live preview (ghosts swing and pose fine — combat truth resumes at the implementer's bake).
- **Any weapon whose silhouette changes becomes a ghost: DELETE its `CIRCLES_V2` row** (row deletion is a sanctioned edit for this brief — and the only one). List every deleted row in your delivery's top-of-file header note. Weapons whose sigil is recolor-class or interior-add keep their rows.
- Redesigns keep the weapon's IDENTITY DATA coherent — see the invariants.

## HARD INVARIANTS (the implementer verifies every one)

- **Zero sim lines, zero renderer lines, zero CFG lines.** (The one historical exception class: owner-directed renderer edits in-session, e.g. the javelin's flight art — only he can grant that, and it must be disclosed `observed:`.)
- **Uniform bases untouched:** every row keeps `omega: 3.5, dmg: 5` and its class ability (+0.7 axis).
- **`len` ≤ LT** (`const LT = maxD(INGR_MACE)` ≈ 113.4 pre-scale). `len` changes only at the owner's in-session word.
- **Traits travel with the art:** if a redesign moves a flagged weapon's business contour — the katana's edge side (`lead: -1`), the scythe's beak side (`lead: -1`), the javelin's head end (`headLead: 1`) — the row's sign flips with it and an `observed:` note says so. If the contour is unchanged, the trait is unchanged.
- **Chain weapons:** `FLAIL_CH`/`NUNCHAKU_CH` (`segs` = shape-index ranges, `j` = joint d-coordinates) must stay consistent with any re-authored shape lists — updating them is art-coupled data and IN scope, with an `observed:` note; the segment structure (counts, hinge order) must not change.
- **Launchers:** the shot leaves from the row's declared point — radial-hold from the tip, tangential from the middle (+`off` along the body radial for `fire:'radial'` kinds). If a redesign moves the mouth/belly, tune `off` to match (the tome/scroll precedent) with an `observed:` note. Shot ART (the `drawShot` kinds) is OUT of scope — the shot-accent companion ships at reconcile.
- **Tangential weapons:** the grip stays at the chord middle (t = 0.5 — the rim point); both ends deliberate (they're visible; no tuck).
- **Palette:** the six shared consts untouched; the scroll's local KT/KTS/KR/KRD stay declared in its zone; `KC` is the only tag you add. Flat fills, ink #2e2b22 added by the renderer, no gradients/texture/glow.
- **WEAPON_IDS frozen** — no additions, no removals, no reordering.
- The file is deterministic: no `Math.random`/`Date.now`/`performance.now` anywhere.

## SANCTIONED ZONES

1. The `INGR_*` art blocks (all 22) — sigils and owner-directed redesigns, with per-weapon header-comment updates.
2. `CIRCLES_V2` — **row deletions only**, for silhouette-changed weapons, listed in the delivery header note.
3. `WEAPONS` rows — art-coupled fields only (`len`, stone `off`, `lead`/`headLead` signs, chain `CH` data references stay pointing at their consts), each change with an `observed:` note in the weapon's header.
4. The scroll zone's local color consts stay where they are; a redesign of the scroll may extend that zone's palette the same disclosed way.

Nothing else. No comment cleanups, no reordering, no formatting sweeps.

## DEFINITION OF DONE

The complete file, boots clean; **every one of the 22 weapons shows its sigil live in the carrier's cast color** (both carriers across rounds); silhouette-changed weapons ghost with the watermark and their rows deleted + listed; untouched-geometry weapons keep valid rows (the gate must still PASS on every non-ghost row — run `.qa/inscribe.mjs` mentally against your own edits: interior paint only); zero diff lines outside the sanctioned zones; `observed:` notes on every judgment call and every row change.

## DELIVERY

Return the complete file. The owner drops it into `deliveries/`; the implementer reconciles: rebake every ghosted weapon (trimmed `--regen`), the inscription gate on all 22, `leadproof` probe re-quotes if any flagged weapon's art moved, the fingerprint ritual (surgical per-weapon deltas), forced-cell gauntlets for changed weapons, the full suite, the shot-accent companion (projectile elements in carrier colors, per kind), and the docs. Then the owner playtests, and the weapon-art convention closes on the approved looks.

### reconcile-runbook.md (verbatim)

# RECONCILE RUNBOOK — full-roster redesign + sigil delivery

Working checklist for the landing of the `roster-redesign-brief.md` commission
(artist session in progress against **build thrash-r11**, md5 `e9662ebf9d038b5d974a5282fad59e8b`).
Written 2026-10-23 while the base is frozen. This file is operational, not a
record — the record entry goes in `GAMES.md` when the reconcile completes, and
this file is then a cleanup candidate.

**Base-freeze rule (why this runbook exists now):** nothing touches
`spinclash-preview.html`, `roster-redesign-brief.md`, or behaviour-describing
doc lines until the delivery lands and is promoted. Two meanwhile-menu items
moved INTO this runbook as a result: the palette redo (step 8a) and the
shot-accent companion (step 8b).

---

## Pre-flight (before opening the delivery)

- [ ] Canonical is still the commissioned base: `md5sum spinclash-preview.html`
      → `e9662ebf9d038b5d974a5282fad59e8b`; `<title>` line 7 → `build thrash-r11`.
      If it moved, the artist worked against a stale base — STOP and disclose.
- [ ] The five STALE-BASE markers re-verified true on canonical (they gate the
      brief at line 21): stamp `thrash-r11` · `CFG.flipMs` 180 · `WEAPON_IDS`
      ends `'orb'` · `KC` present (line 164) · `CFG.reArmDeep` present.
- [ ] Owner uploads the delivery to `deliveries/` VERBATIM (no edits, no
      renames inside the file). Expected name: `spinclash-preview roster.html`
      or the session's own naming — anything is fine, the runbook promotes by
      content, not filename.
- [ ] **Truncation fallback** (agreed in the run card): if the session ran out
      of room, the artist returns ONLY the remaining sanctioned zones and I
      splice them onto canonical. Splice = zone-by-zone replacement of INGR
      blocks + row deletions, each diff-audited as if delivered whole.

## Step 1 — Intake audit

- [ ] Delivery's own `observed:` header notes read in full first (code beats
      comments, but the header is the artist's report — it names every
      disclosed deviation, marker check, and tune).
- [ ] Its recorded STALE-BASE marker check matches the pre-flight above.
- [ ] Verbatim copy retained in `deliveries/` (promotion diff must be provable
      against it; originals are purged only after promotion + owner approval,
      per the round-4 precedent).

## Step 2 — Diff-scope audit

- [ ] Produce a full diff vs canonical (python, exact strings; NEVER anchor
      `edit_file` on `cut -c`-truncated output — the 2026-10-23 doc accident).
- [ ] Classify every hunk. SANCTIONED: art-zone edits (INGR_* / per-weapon draw
      zones), `CIRCLES_V2` ROW DELETIONS (the brief's only sanctioned non-art
      edit — silhouette-changed weapons ghost), `observed:` header lines, and
      any DISCLOSED renderer edit (the javelin `drawJavelin` and scroll-retint
      precedents: allowed, but must be named in the header).
- [ ] UNSANCTIONED (STOP + disclose to owner): sim-code lines, `WEAPON_IDS`
      changes, trait rows edited without art moving, `CFG` changes, the six
      shared palette consts moved by the artist, new mechanics of any kind.

## Step 3 — Invariant audit (the brief's hard list)

- [ ] Traits travel with the art: `lead` / `headLead` flags match the NEW
      business-contour side (katana, scythe, javelin minimum; any redesign that
      moved a contour re-declares).
- [ ] Chain-data coherence: flail + nunchaku segment counts, `NUNCHAKU_CH`,
      `np`, tie data unchanged unless the art provably moved (then disclosed).
- [ ] Launcher `off`/stone config untouched except disclosed tunes with reasons.
- [ ] LT ceiling: no spine longer than LT (≈113.4 pre-scale).
- [ ] Uniform bases; `WEAPON_IDS` frozen at 22, same order.
- [ ] **Sigil doctrine (6):** exactly ONE diegetic element per weapon wears
      `KC`; no invented decoration "for the sake of color"; mark map (~16
      recolors + ~6 smallest-possible interior adds) reconciled row by row.
- [ ] KC-fail-visibly check: any `KC` that reaches paint unswapped is invalid
      CSS — grep the delivery for KC usage outside the sanctioned swap paths.

## Step 4 — Line-ending normalization

- [ ] The recurring stray leading CR: normalize at promotion (every delivery so
      far has had it). Byte-diff after normalization must show content only.

## Step 5 — Ghost-bake prep

- [ ] List silhouette-changed weapons (rows deleted by the artist) vs
      interior-only weapons (rows kept, art changed inside the old silhouette).
- [ ] Interior-only rows are LEFT ALONE — but step 6's gate must still pass
      them; a kept row that now fails means the art crossed the silhouette line
      after all → treat as ghost (delete + rebake) and disclose.
- [ ] Trimmed temp `WEAPON_IDS` → `node .qa/inscribe.mjs --regen` on the
      delivery copy (the batch-4/orb bake pattern; chains bake PER SEGMENT).
- [ ] `n=0 MISS-FAIL` on unbaked rows is EXPECTED pre-bake (the ghost-bake DoD
      — the artist never authors `CIRCLES_V2` rows). It is not a delivery
      defect; record the count and move on.
- [ ] Bake via `.qa/bake-circles-v2.mjs` provenance path (asserts every anchor,
      aborts on mismatch). Disclose any per-weapon floor override (scythe
      precedent: floor 1.8, minR 1.9).

## Step 6 — Inscription gate

- [ ] `node .qa/inscribe.mjs` → PASS on all 22 (poke ≤ 0.12, miss inside house
      margin), chains unchanged (flail 9/13/14/13 0/49 ×3, nunchaku 8/19
      0/27 ×3 — or the new disclosed truth).
- [ ] Zero MISS-FAIL remaining post-bake. Any survivor = visible contact that
      fires nothing → rebake, never loosen the gate.

## Step 7 — Lead/headLead re-quote

- [ ] `.qa/leadproof.mjs`: extend PROBES/CELLS for every flagged weapon whose
      art moved; re-quote the katana/scythe/javelin probe lines in the docs if
      their numbers moved. ALL PASS required.
- [ ] `.qa/flipproof.mjs` ALL PASS (the 180ms transition + d-axis; its seeds
      have survived two pool growths — the rollsFlagged tripwire must stay
      silent; pool is NOT growing here, so any tripwire noise is a real fault).

## Step 8 — Renderer companion (implementer's work, folded in from the menu)

- [ ] **8a. Palette redo (menu item 1, deferred to here).** If the owner still
      wants recognizable stable cast colors, do it NOW on the promoted file,
      not before — the artist's base must not move. Renderer-only ⇒ fingerprint
      8/8 byte-identical proof required. Touches: `COLS` (line ~1722) and the
      sigil swap's fallback (KG gold). Then update brief line 27 ("Speedy's
      gold / Heavy's coral") and the mark map's color names — but note the
      brief is SPENT at this point; the update lands in GAMES.md's doctrine
      text instead, and the brief retires to the archive verbatim.
- [ ] **8b. Shot-accent companion (menu item 2).** Per-kind element accents so
      flying shots carry their weapon's sigil identity: javelin / page / scroll
      / orb / spark / star / boom / bolt. Propose the per-kind element map to
      the owner FIRST (scroll ribbon keeps KR unless he says otherwise), then
      implement in `drawShot` (~2168), `drawJavelin` (2139), `HALO` (2167) —
      `COLS[st.o]` is available; stones carry `o: f.idx`. Renderer-only ⇒
      fingerprint-identical.
- [ ] 8a before 8b if both ship (accents reference the palette).

## Step 9 — Ritual (sim-data change: circles moved)

- [ ] `cp .qa/fp-after.json .qa/fp-pre-roster.json`
- [ ] `node .qa/fingerprint.mjs .qa/fp-post-roster.json`
- [ ] Diff and DIAGNOSE EVERY MOVED CELL before acceptance (tome/scroll
      precedent: naturals re-roll on pool growth — pool is stable here, so any

## Step 10 — Gauntlet + suite

- [ ] Forced gauntlet: `SOLIDITY_CELLS` (23) green; add sigil-specific spot
      cells if any weapon's covering moved materially.
- [ ] Naturals spot-check (`scratch/gatecheck.mjs` pattern retained).
- [ ] Full suite: chromecheck PASS (re-run rule for the documented natural-seed
      timing flake: 4/4 consecutive) · killstill · hitstill · reveal-probe ·
      projectile-fix PROBE PASS (D 4/18 fixed cells) · inscription gate.
- [ ] `measure.mjs` NOT RUN (standing owner-go rule; the sanctioned law re-proof
      stays deferred to the rework window — ~15 sim-data changes + this delivery
      since the 2026-10-19 runs).

## Step 11 — Owner review vehicle + playtest gate

- [ ] `node scratch/sheet-v2.mjs <promoted-file> scratch/roster-sheet-v2.svg`
      (pre-staged companion; `scratch/sheet.mjs` stays the v1 pre-redesign
      vehicle). All 22 weapons at true played scale, rendered TWICE — one pass
      per carrier accent (pass A = the engine's KG fallback, pass B = cast

      **Automated sigil gate: after the delivery, all 44 cells must read
      `sigil KC ×1`.** Pre-delivery they all read `×0 ⚠` (verified on
      thrash-r11: 44/44), since no art emits KC yet — that is the expected

- [ ] Owner playtest of the live game is the FINAL gate on every visual change.
      Watch list to hand him: sigil legibility at speed, the ~5-frame flip
      transition's art≠hitbox window (the one accepted exception), redesigned

## Step 12 — Docs + stamp (only after owner approval)

- [ ] Stamp bump (`<title>` line 7 + `#build` line 83): `build roster-r12`
      (or the next free name).
- [ ] GAMES.md: Measured-facts entry (full numbers), Current-state bullets,

root — proposed lock text per item, a one-page vote sheet with recommendations,

The preview sits at **v1.2** (2026-10-19): the sling is gone — replaced by the **bow** (v0.9) — and a nine-weapon batch (v1.0) plus a shared-length pass (v1.1) grew the roster to **13 weapons, all path silhouettes**; the parts representation is dead. v1.2 is the alignment session: the **projectile rule** (a weapon that fires a shot deals no spine damage — any hold) and the **launch doctrine** (radial hold → tip, tangential hold → middle) were fixed/codified, the mixed-speed shot budget repaired, `measure.mjs` brought to v1.x reality, and the docs re-aligned to the code. Same-day tooling addition (renderer/chrome-only — sim fingerprints identical): a Slow-motion ¼× chip, and the dice reveal is now the owner-designed **VS poster** (his design, copied verbatim at his word, then polished the same day on his playtest notes and its labels regrouped into fighter/weapon blocks — cast disc + written-out skill line over weapon name + ability, owner option 1; landing truth and cadence proven by `.qa/reveal-probe.mjs`). The chrome phase line is removed at owner word (its `chromecheck.mjs` guards moved to the state/canvas surfaces). A later same-day owner tune lengthened the reveal ~1s (`revealTicks` 165 → 285, ~2.4s — the second splits between a ~1.4× slower roll cadence and a longer end hold): a deliberate sim-timeline shift with battle dynamics proven untouched (fingerprint deltas exactly +120 ticks × rounds) and `.qa/fp-after.json` re-baselined. A same-day follow-up tune made the two landings **simultaneous** (`VS.land` 0.32/0.46 → 0.39/0.39, both slams at tick ~111 — owner: "i want oth weapons to appear at the same time"; renderer-only, the sim never reads `VS`, so fingerprints are untouched by construction). A same-day frame-loop addition: **hitstop** — every damage hit briefly freezes time and the killing blow gets a long beat before the K.O. card (trigger policy 1 at owner word: "implement it, and do 1 trigger policy"; renderer-layer, fingerprints proven byte-identical — see **Measured facts**). A same-day sim tune fixed the **parry loop** (machine-gun parries at point-blank): owner option 1 ("1") — `parryPush` 150 → 525 (3.5×), a magnitude-only dial (the laws fix direction and trigger, untouched), the worst loop cell halved its parries and ended ~6.4k ticks sooner; fingerprints re-baselined (see **Measured facts**).
- **K.O. kill still shipped; the inscribe fix landed (2026-10-19).** Owner pick "1" built the renderer-only kill still (frozen K.O. frames draw the killing blow's stored contact poses; fingerprints byte-identical — proofs and shape in **Measured facts**); the still stays (owner never said `r`). Building it corrected the diagnosis of his seed-63230 screenshot: the visible gap is not tick-end drift (measured sub-3px) but **covering circles poking past the weapon inks**. His follow-up made visible-contact-first a standing top priority ("this should be a top priority to always make weapons visibly touch something else before something happens, like nobody wants to see parry fire or body taking damage when there is a visible gap between etc") and the fix landed the same day: **CIRCLES_V2** — all 13 weapons' coverings regenerated union-inscribed in the drawn ink (raster-EDT audit + house greedy packing; poke now ≤ −0.3px everywhere — was hammer +13.6/axe +8.5/bow +2.0/dagger +1.5/cover-batch ≤ +0.26 — and the disclosed whiff band shrank from 13.1px to ≤ ~4.4px on tapered tips), plus renderer-only **halo discs** so every shot kind inks its own hitbox. Sim-data change: fingerprints re-baselined with the delta proof, suite all green (its entry is in the archive retirement; the tombstone carries the doctrine). `.qa/inscribe.mjs` is the **permanent inscription gate** — run after any art/poly/weaponScale edit and for every new weapon. *(Full records of both — and of the other nine retired with them — are verbatim in `CHRONICLE-archive.md`, "Retired at the 2026-10-23 hygiene pass"; the tombstone carrying their permanent outcomes opens **Measured facts**.)*
- **v1.2 baselines recorded; the forced matrix is deferred.** Two sanctioned runs on 2026-10-19, every law check green in both: **part 0** (40 naturals + determinism — determinism PASS, all 13 law checks PASS over 6.4M substep poses, the three balance reds recorded: 39/40 termination, stall worst 7.50s, one 420s battle) and the owner's **`--solidity` overlap suite** (76 battles in 7m18s — law-3 worst transient 5.568px, inside the disclosed band, logged as a watch item) — see **Measured facts**. The 169-cell matrix was attempted the same day and abandoned by owner decision: the assistant's sandbox cannot host matrix-scale runs (long runs get killed and look like stalls), and the matrix is the balance-pass instrument anyway — it is one command on the owner's machine. **`measure.mjs` never runs without the owner's explicit go** (standing rule, 2026-10-19).

One-line fix (exact bound); sim-data change — fingerprints re-baselined with the delta proof (3/8 cells byte-identical, 5 chaotic drifts), the one new 150s-cap hit diagnosed as divergence not re-buzz (heavy wins hammer+dagger s5 at 164.0s, worst 1s parry window 4), fast-tier suite all green including the budget law at the new k values (0/238,781). Details in **Measured facts**.
- **Flail shipped into the canonical preview (2026-10-21, owner: "maybe you should implement the fixes in our spinclash preview instead of the flail 1.html?").** The Claude flail delivery (proportion iterations v1–v5 on the delivered file, syntax-checks only) was PROMOTED: the delivered file became the canonical `spinclash-preview.html` verbatim (diff proof: zero non-flail sim lines), then its covering was baked per segment — four temp regens, one per segment's ink, merged into one 49-circle `CIRCLES_V2.flail` row whose boot tagging ties every circle to its segment (tie v2 since 2026-10-22: centre-in-outer-ink, innermost first, loud desync throw — v1's whole-circle solid-ink test silently parked 46/49 circles on the haft and the hitbox rode a phantom straight chain whenever the links bent; see Measured facts) — and the inscription gate went green on it (poke −0.29px, miss 2.80px, inside the disclosed band). The flail now damages, parries and gets parried like any melee weapon, with its chain articulation live (forced-cell sanity: flail+hammer 28 hits/28 parries, mace+flail 50/28, flail mirror terminates). The owner's two playtest complaints were both the UNBAKED state, not bugs: no parry/no damage is the art-preview ghost rule by design, and the bow's parallel arrow stream is the fixed release phase of a once-per-revolution launcher that nothing flips (the ghost flail could not parry it) — both cured by the bake. Sim change: fingerprints re-baselined; delta shape = every forced cell tick-identical with the hash differing only by the inert per-fighter `fl` chain-state field the mechanics carry on all fighters (zero non-flail diff lines + identical ticks prove behaviour untouched), naturals re-rolled because the dice pool grew 13→14 (s11 ends 17214 vs cap). Fast tier green; the probe's informational termination red reads 4/18 this run. DISCLOSED GAP: `measure.mjs` law audits recompute contact poses from event data, which stores no chain pose — flail cells are unauditable by the laws until the harness grows a chain-pose channel (flagged for the balance pass, not built). Delivery file removed after promotion (2026-10-22, owner cleanup): the promotion was verbatim, so the canonical preview itself is the source record; `deliveries/` stays as the intake point for the next commission.
- **Generic-handheld grammar prescriptive + crossbow rescaled (2026-10-21, owner).** Four design lines for every future weapon written into **Weapon art** as prescriptive owner additions: length ceiling `LT` (the heavy-four shared length — "it looks good for the eye"), one shaft size with a disclosed-exception clause, plain-wood butt (`#7a4a2a`, no patterns), flat butt cut (the tuck hides it). The crossbow — the one spine past the ceiling (len 116) — was rescaled to LT the same day ("fix the crossbow"): uniform downscale of poly and baked covering at boot, surgical fingerprint proof (7/8 cells byte-identical), inscription gate green. Body-solidity for no-damage spines was considered and **rejected** the same day (open question 10 — the pass-through shove is what teaches the projectile rule).
- **Movement-speed doctrine written AND built (2026-10-21, owner; build ordered same day: "implement it in the game for me to see it and review").** A new normative section beside Uniform base stats: **cruise speed 250** as a never-below floor that never steers (direction stays authored by gravity and contact only — the owner's falling-straight-down prohibition), collision-only **excess** with a **0.5s settle half-life**, ballistics untouched so the approved arcs survive verbatim. Dials delegated to the assistant and picked viewer-first; both join open question 1 pending first playtest. Spin growth sources (skill + ability) re-confirmed as balance-pass material. Built as an excess ledger (impulse sites register, half-life drains; walls mirror, floor re-anchors — the bounce stays ballistic by disclosed refinement); verified 0/437k living fight ticks under cruise, four previously capped fingerprint cells terminated, fast tier green; **playtested and REVERTED the same day** (owner: "it does not look natural anymore like it did before") — the sim is back to pre-doctrine ballistics, revert proven byte-identical; the doctrine survives as a design record only (see its section header).
- **Parry flutter gate built (2026-10-21, owner option 1).** The seed-14052 playtest's 4-clang vibrato burst (legal parries at +12/+4/+10 ticks in the bottom cradle) became law 2's second sanctioned extension: after a parry, the latch cannot re-arm for 16 ticks — contacts inside the gate stay fully solid but fire nothing, so the burst reads clang-bind-separate instead of a buzz. Bimodal gap data made 16 a clean cut (flutter ≤16 vs legit p10 81); post-gate: gaps ≤16 went 51 → 0, bursts 1.8 → 0.3 per battle. Sim change, fingerprints re-baselined, fast tier green; residual double-taps at ≥17 ticks are the dial's review material (`CFG.parryGate`). Same day: the **pin-escape whip** (owner option 1) — built (drained angular kick instead of the one-frame teleport the owner's eye caught on seed 79635), playtested, and **REVERTED the same day**: the drain re-buried blades at the resolver's push-out rate and glued pinned pairs into sustained grapples. The instant nudge is restored verbatim; the warp stays a disclosed blemish with three candidate cures on record (law-3 caveat). The flutter gate stands. Later the same day (owner go): **parryPush 525 → 700** for smoother, more natural separation, and candidate (a) adopted in tick-gated form — the pin nudge capped at 12px-at-the-head, one add per tick, so escapes sweep smoothly over ~3–12 frames; verified with the duration metric (0 visible overlaps, 0/20 glued battles). Same day, the **chaos ceiling + wall bleed** (owner option 1 from a measured menu): cap 1100 → 1200 and walls absorb 10% per contact — top-rim touches 0.95 → 3.05 per battle at +3% pace (p90 pinned at the old cap); a cap-1600 version was playtest-rejected ("too fast for the eye") and reverted byte-identically before this landed. Same day, at owner word, the **bounce feel tune**: gravity 1500 → 1400 ("bounce more, not faster" — higher, hangier arcs at identical speeds).
- **Deep-disengage gate built (2026-10-23, owner option 1 — "do 1" with N=90).** Law 4's re-arm side got its sanctioned extension: re-arm needs the 12px pad AND (≥85px peak clearance OR ≥90 ticks out). Kills the seed-24267 thrash volley (javelin re-entering 3× in 95 ticks — hits now 95/114 ticks apart) and the population-wide shallow classes (thrash-shaped pairs 0/701; middle-band fast pairs 8 → 0), with genuine exchanges provably untouched (the [80,85) band is empty; every surviving <90t pair clears ≥91px). Sim-data change: fingerprints re-baselined, `measure.mjs` law-4 replica mirrored (edited, NOT run — owner-go rule), suite green; stamp **build thrash-r11**; the redesign brief's STALE-BASE markers moved with it. One in-session calibration correction: the first-pass 48px threshold (calibrated on the naturals' ≤21px class) provably missed the owner's episode — its true substep peaks rock 69–79px — so the threshold sits above the whole middle band. Numbers and proofs in **Measured facts**. **Playtest pending.**
- **The weapon art convention is NOT locked** (owner, 2026-10-19: "the art 'convention' is not locked, as i have not decided yet"). What the art currently does is described, not prescribed — see **Weapon art**.
- **Balance is deferred** (open question 1) until the mechanics set is declared complete. Known reds are recorded, not judged: the shield cannot damage (shield+shield never terminates), pure-projectile mirror cells routinely pass 300s, the stall alarm was red at the last full audit.

### Weapon art — the descriptive section as it stood through roster-r12


**Landed state (read this first; the history below is record):** the full-roster redesign + sigil commission landed 2026-10-25 through the artist-kit route (returns #1–#2 in `deliveries/`, promotion chain `e9662ebf…` → `59b87b17…` → `dccb4892…` → `3bc1fbee…` → stamp `d09ffc12…`): 22 weapon blocks in canonical `build([...])` grammar pre-scale, 19 coverings rebaked by `.qa/bake-rows.mjs`, chain segs re-mapped, sigils realized one-KC-per-weapon, nine shot kinds in `SHOTS_ART` with the affinity locals, the knives volley and the tome trio rotation in the sim, copy-kind throws at centroid pivots. The grammar described below (flat fills, locked palette + locals, legibility lock, shaft doctrine, prescriptive handheld spec, lead/headLead taxonomy) is now PRESCRIPTIVE from the landed art; the close draft ratifies it item by item. `ingest.mjs` stands dormant/superseded. What follows is the section as it stood through the landing, kept as record.

What follows **describes current practice**; none of it is prescribed. The 2026-10-18 locked convention (palette of five, shaft doctrine, per-family proportions, supplier/selector process, squash test) is preserved verbatim in the archive — its amber `#c9a227` no longer appears in any merged art, and the v1.0 batch did not go through its process.

**What the art currently does.** Flat fills; one uniform ink outline (`#2e2b22`); no gradients, no texture, no glow. Colors in use: wood `#7a4a2a`, dark wood `#4f3220`, steel `#8fa3b5`, near-white `#eaf6ff` (cutting/striking faces — and PAPER: the tome's pages, owner-named extension), and light blue `#9fe0ff` (the orb body, the tome gem, the scroll sparkles). **Gold is RETIRED from art (sweep-r15, owner option B, 2026-10-27): every former gold shape — 21 `KG`-tagged plus 7 ingest-era hexes — now wears dark wood `#4f3220`; `KG` survives only as the carrier-less sigil fallback constant.** Circular elements are cut as 32-gons (roundness class, same day: nine true circles recut to 32; elongated wraps are exempt by the aspect gate and keep their authored contours). The scroll (2026-10-23) adds the first PER-WEAPON owner-reference extension: KT `#ecd5a3` parchment, KTS `#c9a35a`, KR `#b8352c` ribbon, KRD `#8f2a24` — declared scroll-local inside its INGR zone, the six shared consts untouched; **the convention close must ratify (or veto) the per-weapon reference-color class.** Squint-test-at-120px and minimum-feature-15/1000 were the old legibility rules; nobody has re-checked the v1.0 batch against them.

**Owner additions 2026-10-21 — PRESCRIPTIVE for every future batch and every new weapon (the rest of this section stays descriptive until the convention itself locks): the generic handheld weapon spec.** (1) **Length ceiling:** no weapon spine is longer than `LT` — the shared heavy-four length (the mace's tip, ≈113.4 pre-scale, ≈147.5px as played). Owner: "a max length of weapons, where a weapon can not be longer than this, per default … its about the lenght of spear,axe hammer … it looks good for the eye". Skills that grow length later are a mechanics topic, never an exception to the authored rest length. The crossbow was the one spine past the ceiling (len 116) and was rescaled to LT the same day at owner word ("fix the crossbow") — uniform downscale, proportions untouched (sim-data change; **Measured facts**). (2) **One shaft size:** generic handheld weapons share the shaft doctrine's universal width (~11.5u full width, ~15 as played); a deviation is only a named, disclosed owner pick for a very specific design — the exception mechanism already exists as practice (axe2-D's 13.5u haft, chosen on the true-scale sheet, "owner taste overrides the doctrine default"). (3) **Plain butt:** the handle bottom the bot wields is the handle brown `#7a4a2a` with no patterns and no other colors — this settles the 2026-10-18 palette's dark-wood "butts, grips" assignment against merged practice (every merged butt is wood; the axe's gold pommel/collar left at v0.8.4). Owner: "the bottom of the handle where the weapon is wielded by the bot should just be brown color as we are using in most of the handles right now, with no patterns/design other colors". (4) **Flat butt cut:** the shaft bottom is cut flat, not round — the tuck clip hides it in play anyway ("the bottom of the shaft should not be round, as it is going under the bots body anyyways, so nobody will see it"); the two places it IS visible (VS poster, contact sheets — neither tuck-clips) read a flat cut as a normal handle end. The v1.0 builder batch was already flat; the three ingested veterans (hammer, dagger, axe) keep their rounded caps until their next art touch — grandfathered, not re-authored for this rule. (5) **Directional asymmetry declares `lead`** (owner decision 2026-10-22 — the katana "appears to spin backwards" observation): art with a directional reading — a chisel blade, a hook, a curve whose **business contour** (the cutting edge of a blade, the BEAK of a hook) looks to one side of the spine — declares `lead: ±1` on its WEAPONS row (the spine-local side that contour bears), and the placement layer mirrors art+hitbox together on spin sign so the contour leads the motion at both spin directions and through every parry flip. Katana and scythe are both −1, the reference implementations of the two contour classes — **for hooks the owner's playtest is the ruling doctrine: shipped mouth-forward (+1), it read as "spinning the wrong way from the beginning"; the beak must lead, the mouth scoops behind.** **Radial asymmetry never declares it** — art whose off-axis mass faces away from the body (the bow/crossbow bellies, the knives' outward blades) would mirror inward, wrong at both signs; symmetric art declares nothing; chain weapons are forbidden it by boot throw (mirror × tie unproven until a real case). Artists author in the canonical orientation — edge on the declared side — and the engine flips; `.qa/leadproof.mjs` extends (PROBES/CELLS) for every new flagged weapon. *

(5b) **Along-chord asymmetry declares `headLead`** (owner decision 2026-10-22 — "i think the javelin should also flip around like scythe and katana"): a weapon whose BUSINESS END sits at one chord extreme (a spear head) declares `headLead: ±1` (the d-end bearing the head; the javelin's is the d = len end, +1), and the placement layer d-mirrors art+hitbox about the **centred rim grip** — the chord midpoint, the mirror's fixed point, which is why the trait is tangential-hold-ONLY (a radial grip at d 0 would slide off the body; boot throw) — so the head always points the way the grip travels (kinematics: v·d̂ = bodyR·spin). One flip factor drives one declared axis: `lead` XOR `headLead`, never both, never on chains (boot throws). The javelin rider's "bilaterally symmetric, no lead" guidance stays true — its asymmetry is along the chord, not across it, which is exactly the case `lead` cannot fix and `headLead` exists for. (6) **The sigil — one carrier-colored element per weapon** (owner decision 2026-10-23, his words: "i dont want it to be like all weapons have the same dot or similar, i want it to make sense for the weapons … dont just invent something on the sword just for the sake of givinig it a color, like put a string or something stupid on the sword, which doesnt make sense"): exactly ONE diegetic element per weapon renders in the wielding fighter's cast color via the `KC` tag (recolor an existing plausible element first — band, gem, hub, binding, seal; add the smallest plausible ownership element only where none exists, interior-ink preferred; never on KP; owner-reference colors like the scroll's KR ribbon are not sigil material). The mark map lives in `roster-redesign-brief.md`; the mechanism is the `drawWeapon`/`drawWeaponChain` swap (renderer-only, gold fallback for carrier-less renders). Sigil = the vocabulary word (SPEC's "mark" stays reserved for body identity).

**Two art paths coexist — RESOLVED at roster-r12 (close item 2a): the in-file poly builder via the artist-kit route IS the canonical path; `ingest.mjs` stands dormant/superseded. (2026-10-25 alignment note; the subsection below is the record as it stood.)**

**Two art paths coexist (undecided which is canonical):**
1. **`ingest.mjs` — the SVG merge gate:** validate → normalize → cover (0.5u grid, ink inflated 1.5u = the drawn outline counted as body, RMIN 2.0, ε disclosed) → combat gauntlet on a temp copy → merge only on PASS. Produced `INGR_HAMMER`, `INGR_DAGGER`, `INGR_AXE` (all with gauntlet records in the archive) and `INGR_BOW` (blob shape matches; **no gauntlet record**). Its merge template hardcodes the melee ability (+0.7 DMG) — a launcher candidate needs a post-merge edit. Its orientation rule: an end tapering to ~zero width is the tip; only when neither end tapers does the thinner blunt end read as the grip.
2. **The in-file `build()` poly builder (v1.0 batch):** weapons authored as plain polygons in spine-local (d, l) units; covering circles generated from the same polygons (1.5u sample grid, silhouette inflated **1.2u**, min r **4**, dedupe 0.72) — different parameters from ingest, **no ε disclosure, no gauntlet** for any of the nine. Drawing and hitbox still cannot drift (same source polys), but uncovered-sliver depth is unmeasured.

**Undecided (open question):** palette (**settled at owner word 2026-10-27: gold retired from art (sweep to KD, sweep-r15), blue IN, amber long retired — convention-close item 1a carries the amended lock**), the process (batches + contact sheets + owner-picks, or direct authoring?), whether the builder path must pass the ingest gate retroactively, per-family proportions beyond the decided ceiling and shaft size (head shares, guard treatment — see the archive grammar). The owner decides; until then new art follows whichever path the owner's next pick implies, inside the prescriptive spec above.

**Commission convention, written down (2026-10-22, owner word: "remember to write down our weapon convention").** The convention was written down that day as two paste-ready Claude-artist briefs at the repo root — `weapon-artist-brief.md` (general rigid-weapon commission — owner inserts name/design/class; OUTPUT RULES never-images/never-ask/complete-file, BASE FILE TRUTH with the single `|| []` self-heal and code-beats-comments, the prescriptive handheld spec — LT ceiling, ~11.5u shaft, plain flat butt, six-color flat palette — sanctioned zones, MECHANICS INVARIANTS, ghost-bake definition of done) and `nunchaku-artist-brief.md` (the chain-rider pattern, prefilled for the nunchaku: 2-segment authoring, `NUNCHAKU_CH = {segs, j:[J0,TIP]}` shape, the tie-v2 art rules distilled from the flail's phantom-chain bug, artist scope = art + data with ZERO sim lines). **CORRECTED 2026-10-23 (doc↔workspace defect found during the base-freeze wait): both files were deleted the same day at the owner's cleanup order (round 2: "also remove earclacks preview.html as well as the weapon artist brief"), and the four single-weapon rider briefs were purged at round 4 — so no standing brief file exists at root and this paragraph's "live at the repo root again … the operative text for every commission session" was stale.** The house pattern (round 4): every commission gets a FRESH rider brief built against current canonical — its STALE-BASE markers, sanctioned zones, invariants and ghost-bake definition of done re-derived from the file, never reused from a spent brief; `roster-redesign-brief.md` is the live instance (in progress at this writing, retires verbatim to the archive at its landing). The prescriptive spec above is embedded verbatim in every rider; the section content of the two deleted general briefs survives only as this description. Same day the canonical base was pre-wired N-segment chain-generic (`ch.np`, fingerprints byte-identical — **Measured facts**, last entry), so the nunchaku rider's STALE-BASE marker check passes against current canonical.

Reconciled per pipeline: bake 19 circles (minR 2.80 — the roster's highest, zero budget cost; poke −0.30, miss 2.46), gate PASS all 19, gauntlet green (projectile rule holds: spine 0 blade hits; the three caps are the known projectile-mirror red class — javelin joins it), ritual: 6 forced cells byte-identical, naturals re-rolled (pool 18→19; s7 rolls javelin+javelin and holds the cap, s11 → 8888), fast tier all green, `SOLIDITY_CELLS` 18→19 (+javelin+javelin), matrix counts 361. A stray leading CR from transfer was normalized at promotion; the delivery original stays in `deliveries/`. Full record in **Measured facts**; **owner playtest is the final gate** (the flight art's head leads its hitbox disc by ~2.5 head-lengths — the arrows' safe-direction ink-leads-the-hit class, bigger by design).
- **Javelin head-flip shipped — the `headLead` trait (2026-10-22, owner playtest of the merged javelin: "i think the javelin should also flip around like scythe and katana").** The javelin's asymmetry runs ALONG the chord (head at the d = len end), which the lateral `lead` mirror cannot fix by construction — so the sibling trait: `headLead: ±1`, a d-mirror pivoting about the centred rim grip (the mirror's fixed point — tangential-only, lead XOR headLead, no chains, all by loud boot throws), riding the SAME one flip factor, the SAME 180ms transition, and the SAME event spin fields (the `aSp`/`bSp`/`fSp` conditions widened to both traits). Symptom proven before the build: unmirrored, spin<0 javelins travel tail-first at 64/64 angles (v·d̂ = bodyR·spin). Sim-data change: fingerprints re-baselined, **7/8 cells byte-identical — only natural s7 (the javelin mirror) moved**; leadproof extended to the d-axis (256/256, mapping 72/72, Part B + javelin parries/swats), flipproof's control seed moved s7 → s1 (s7 now rolls flagged); suite green. Full record in **Measured facts**; the taxonomy addition (5b) is prescriptive in **Weapon art**.
- **Javelin commissioned — design locked, rider paste-ready (2026-10-22, owner: "i wanna start with javelin and it should be thrown" → "i agree, tangential and also with the throw direction, dont bother about skills stats/heavy profile, etc for now").** Tangential launcher at `len: LT` (the longest rim chord on the roster — a spear crossing the rim), released tangentially in the spin direction (the boomerang/shuriken family: no `fire`/`off` in the stone config), straight flight, **zero new law surface**; stats and the shot profile deferred to the balance pass at owner word — the row ships class-default and provisional (ω 3.5 / dmg 5 / +0.7 SPD; stone r 7 / 540 / len 46). No `lead` (the art is bilaterally symmetric about the spine by brief). The dedicated `javelin` shot kind is **pre-wired** — renderer-only, inert until the weapon row lands, fingerprints 8/8 byte-identical — so the delivery previews complete with zero renderer edits at reconcile (the knives-borrowed-bolt-art flag, not repeated). `javelin-artist-brief.md` at the repo root, rebuilt against current canonical with the STALE-BASE markers, the ghost-bake DoD and the verbatim row; pool grows to 19 at merge. Full record in **Measured facts**.
- **Edge-leading mirror shipped — katana + scythe (2026-10-22, owner observation: "katana, since it has a sharp side on its blade, the weapon appears to spin backwards … i was thinking if we could flip the weapon when its turn").** Directionally asymmetric art declares the spine-local side bearing its **business contour** (`lead: ±1` — the cutting edge of a chisel blade, the BEAK of a hook; katana and scythe are both −1 — the scythe shipped first at +1, mouth-forward, and the owner's playtest read it "spinning the wrong way from the beginning", flipped the same day); the placement layer mirrors art AND hitbox together on spin sign, so the contour always leads the motion at both fighters and through every parry flip (law 6 guarantees one fighter per round spun each way — without the mirror one blade always travelled backwards). One source (`leadFlip`, exported on `geom`) feeds the sim's collision, the renderer, the stills and every harness recompute; events carry the flip truth (`aSp`/`bSp` entry-signed / `fSp`, flagged weapons only — unflagged event JSON byte-identical); chains forbidden the flag by boot throw; the VS poster stays canonical. Sim-data change: fingerprints re-baselined, **7/8 cells byte-identical** (only natural s11 diverged — its mace+scythe round); birth proof `.qa/leadproof.mjs` ALL PASS; suite green with the stills/measure recomputes made flip-aware in the same pass (the chain-blind lesson applied preemptively). Disclosed: the instantaneous mirror pop on parry ticks, consistent with the recoil's own snap — **playtest verdict: the orientation works ("good its working"); the snap itself was then replaced at owner word — "i thought something else, like a fast transition not an instant snap"** — the flagged blade now eases old → edge-on → mirrored over `CFG.flipMs` 180ms (raised from 90: "its still too snappy") like a spin about its own spine (art-only, fight-phase-only, generic for every future `lead` weapon; the sim, laws, stills and the hitbox overlay keep the binary truth — fingerprints 8/8 byte-identical, `.qa/flipproof.mjs` ALL PASS; the disclosed ~11-frame (180ms) art≠hitbox window per flagged parry is the owner-accepted trade — Measured facts). The two invisible glint iterations were removed with it, and the **build stamp** convention stays — now `build flip-r4` in the header/title, bumped on every owner-visible change. The queue remaining: **tome** — designed in chat (tangential open book, not wide open, pages outward; owner: "i was actually thinking making the tome tangential, and then make it look like an open book, with pages (not wide open)"), rider next — then scroll (burst:3 — the burst-shape decision comes first), magic orb (orbiting sentinel — the tethered-vs-sentinel design session comes first).

### Measured-facts entries dated up to 2026-10-23

**REACH fix (2026-10-21, owner go — resolves the code-review pass's one reported-not-changed item — that pass's entry is retired verbatim to `CHRONICLE-archive.md` at the 2026-10-23 hygiene pass; sim-data change, fingerprints re-baselined).** The radial-hold `REACH` term inflated the 1.5px ink half-stroke laterally INSIDE the hypotenuse (`hypot(d, |l|+1.5)`) instead of radially OUTWARD (`hypot(d,l)+1.5` — the tangential branch's own recipe), under-stating tip-most reach by ≤ ~1.2–1.5px. One-line fix; the new form is an exact bound on the ink-offset solid (its farthest point is a vertex distance + 1.5). Both consumers — the parryGap prune and the substep tip-speed budgets — carried documented slack (≥1.6px prune margin vs ≤2×~1.2px shortfall; ≤2.5% budget spend), so no baseline was ever red and no contact was ever pruned away (δA+δB ≤ ~3.0 < parryPad 4 by arithmetic): the margin simply stops being eaten. **No harness edits:** measure.mjs and projectile-fix.mjs audit budgets off the sim's own `auditK` records, and the probe's independent recompute replicates only the shot-vs-shot term (REACH-free). Delta proof (`.qa/fp-pre-reach.json` → `.qa/fp-post-reach.json`): **3/8 cells byte-identical** (bow+crossbow s2, boomerang+shuriken s1, natural s7 — the +1.5 never crossed a ceil boundary in their k) · 5 drifted as k shifted at boundaries — chaotic granularity re-rolls, no bounded form: hammer+dagger s5 9388→18000 (the 150s cap), staff+hammer s1 7946→8644, shield+hammer s4 11038→11181, natural s11 7816→10125, wand+bow s3 holds the cap (hash moved). **The one new cap-hit was diagnosed before acceptance** (throwaway 420s-budget trace): hammer+dagger s5 now runs a full three-round series — heavy wins at 164.0s, 57 hits / 55 parries, worst 1s parry window **4** (the pre-pp525 machine-gun signature was dozens in a burst): divergence, not re-buzz. Suite: chromecheck PASS · inscription gate PASS (numbers unchanged — not an art edit) · reveal-probe ALL PASS with zero edits · killstill ALL PASS (22 kills / 14 seeds, gContact ≤0.3 on 22/22; split drifted blade 14 / stone 8) · projectile-fix PROBE PASS — A 0 melee hits by projectile weapons (non-vacuity: 108 melee-side / 610 landings / 1512 shove edges), B 0/6628 launches off-doctrine, **C 0/238,781 budget ticks under-provisioned at the new k values** (the REACH-relevant law), D informational termination red moved 7/18 → 5/18 (mirror-length reds are seed-dependent — open question 1). `.qa/fp-after.json` re-baselined := post bytes; pre retained at `.qa/fp-pre-reach.json`, post record at `.qa/fp-post-reach.json`. `measure.mjs` NOT run (owner-go rule) — the full law re-proof rides with the next sanctioned run.

**Hit still (2026-10-21, owner option 1 — every damage hit's freeze shows its touch; renderer-only, fingerprints byte-identical and proven: `.qa/fp-pre-hitstill.json` = `.qa/fp-post-hitstill.json` = `.qa/fp-after.json`).** Born from the owner's seed-12024 playtest ("as the mace left the body somehow heavy took damage again … it looked like it was damaged twice"): the exact-battle replay proved both hits legal with 4.1s of visible separation between them (max clearance 587px — bodies across the arena), but the killing blow's contact lived **entirely between drawn frames** — a substep graze (detection per substep, renderer draws tick poses; zero visible overlap at every tick pose of its own tick, ~20px/tick relative speed entering and leaving the disc inside 8.3ms). Live motion therefore showed flash + HP move with no touch; the touch in the owner's K.O. screenshot was the kill still's stored contact pose, which the moving frames never drew. The kill still owned the K.O. hold already; the hole was every freeze before it. Fix: the kill still's mechanism generalised to non-kill hits — every damage hit latches its stored contact poses (`hitStill`), the draw overrides the view with them while that hit's hitstop freeze is in flight (~60ms, 4 drawn frames at 60fps), and stone landings freeze the shot at its impact point. Class size (`.qa/hitstill.mjs` Part A, 15 seeds, 414 non-kill hits): blade contacts invisible at both drawn poses of their tick **1/168** (the graze class — rare but real, and the owner's kill was one); stone landings **246/246** were never drawn poses at all (spliced on death — their impact read only as flash + vanishing shot); gContact ≤ 0.3 on **414/414** latched poses. Part B (seed 12024 through the real DOM-stubbed frame loop): latch bit-equal to the event poses, stable across the 4 freeze frames, null on the first unfrozen frame. Disclosed: resume snaps from contact pose to tick-end pose (≤3px on deep hits, a few px on grazes, masked by flash decay); a same-tick kill + non-kill pair shows the kill's contact during the long freeze while the other victim still flashes. The kill-freeze staging itself (victim already faded at impact, flash at half strength — the death mark starts at the moment of death) was put to the owner as a same-day follow-up and **left as shipped** (owner, 2026-10-21: "no it is fine now i think"). Suite: chromecheck PASS · killstill ALL PASS (22/22, unchanged) · inscription gate PASS · reveal-probe ALL PASS · projectile-fix PROBE PASS (numbers identical to the REACH baseline — sim untouched) · fingerprint byte-identical. `measure.mjs` NOT run (owner-go rule).

**Crossbow rescale to the length ceiling (2026-10-21, owner word "fix the crossbow" alongside the generic-handheld grammar; sim-data change, fingerprints re-baselined).** The crossbow's len 116 was the single overshoot of the new ceiling (`LT`, the heavy-four shared length ≈113.4 pre-scale); fix = uniform downscale by KX = LT/116 ≈ 0.9779 — poly at boot beside the v1.1 loop (proportions and grip centring untouched), the baked CIRCLES_V2 covering takes the same factor at boot (a uniform scale of an inscribed covering stays inscribed). **Parry flutter gate (2026-10-21, owner option 1 — law 2's second sanctioned extension; sim-data change, fingerprints re-baselined).** Owner playtest seed 14052: a 4-clang vibrato burst (parries +12/+4/+10 ticks, bottom cradle, wall-assisted) — legal but "ugly for the viewer". Measurement first (throwaway trace): 36 such bursts (≥2 parries ≤20 ticks apart) across 20 naturals = 1.8 per battle; re-parry gaps bimodal — flutter cluster ≤16 ticks (51 gaps), legit cluster p10 81. Fix: `parryGate` 16 — after a fired parry the latch cannot re-arm for 16 ticks; contacts inside stay fully solid (resolver + approach-kill) but fire nothing. Delta proof (`.qa/fp-pre-flutter.json` → `.qa/fp-post-flutter.json`): 0/8 identical (parry timing moves every timeline); four capped cells terminated (hammer+dagger 18000→16434, shield+hammer 11181→10729, natural s11 10125→9663, staff+hammer 8644→7844), bow+crossbow s2 / wand+bow s3 / natural s7 hold the cap; no pathology. Post-gate scan: gaps ≤16: 51 → **0**, ≤20: 53 → 7, bursts 36 → 5 (0.3/battle — residuals are clean double-taps at ≥17 ticks; seed 14052's burst is now a 2-clang double-tap at +17); total re-parry gaps 921 → 800 (the ~120 removed are the flutter re-fires). Suite: chromecheck PASS · killstill ALL PASS (23 kills / 14 seeds, gContact 23/23) · hitstill ALL PASS (445/445 gContact ≤ 0.3) · inscription gate PASS · reveal-probe ALL PASS · projectile-fix PROBE PASS (B 0/6439, C 0/238,621, D informational 6/18). `.qa/fp-after.json` re-baselined := post; pre at `.qa/fp-pre-flutter.json`, post record at `.qa/fp-post-flutter.json`. `measure.mjs` NOT run (owner-go rule) — the law 2/5 audits are structurally unaffected (fired parries still recompute to exact touch; spins still flip only at fired parries). **Pin-escape whip (2026-10-21, owner option 1 — "as long as it looks good and natural for the eye it is fine"; sim-data change, fingerprints re-baselined).** Owner playtest seed 79635: the resolver's disclosed pin-escape fired as a one-substep angle teleport — ±0.178 rad, ~36px of mace head in a single drawn frame, ~3.2 such warps per battle across 20 naturals. Fix: the escape rotation is queued as a pending kick and drained at `kickHalfLife` 0.012s (~3 drawn frames of continuous sweep), one queue-add per tick per episode, pending capped 0.6 rad, per-add capped at 20px-at-the-head (`kickPx` / REACH). Build-time A/B against the law-3 band: instant escape = 0.19% overlap / 1.2px but the owner-rejected warp; slow drains (0.03s) or ungated re-adds stacked whips into prolonged deep grinds (9.6% / 7.15px VISIBLE — rejected); the shipped 0.012s + per-tick gate = 9.4% of fight-ticks at **worst 2.3px — entirely sub-ink** (inside the 3px outline: on screen the inks cross, i.e. the contact is visible; zero visible interpenetration, zero teleports). Law 3's acceptance split accordingly into visible (>2.5px: old line, measure.mjs reports both classes) and the disclosed sub-ink whip transient. Delta proof (`.qa/fp-pre-whip.json` → `.qa/fp-post-whip.json`): 3/8 byte-identical (the pin-free cells — the whip touches only pin episodes), 5 drifted (hammer+dagger s5 10311→13422, staff+hammer s1 7735→9379; caps hold). Suite: chromecheck PASS · killstill ALL PASS (19/19) · hitstill ALL PASS (404/404) · inscription gate PASS · reveal-probe ALL PASS · projectile-fix PROBE PASS (C 0/233,310, D informational 4/18). `.qa/fp-after.json` re-baselined := post; pre at `.qa/fp-pre-whip.json`, post at `.qa/fp-post-whip.json`. `measure.mjs` edited (law-3 visible/sub-ink split) but NOT run (owner-go rule). Dials for the owner's eye: `kickHalfLife`, `kickPx`; instant-escape revert path = both to zero. **Playtest pending — the owner's condition was that it look good and natural.** **Playtest verdict (same day): REJECTED — seed 79635 showed the pair glued in a sustained grapple ("now they are both stuck together like in that position, never separating"): the whip's drained rotation re-buried the blades at the resolver's push-out rate and the per-tick re-add fed it — a self-sustaining pin. Reverted in full the same day: revert proven byte-identical to `.qa/fp-pre-whip.json`, `fp-after.json` restored to those bytes, the whip-era sim recorded at `.qa/fp-post-whip.json`. The one-frame pin warp on long weapons is back as the disclosed blemish (~3.2/battle); candidate cures recorded under the Core contact law's caveat, none adopted.**

**Parry separation raise + px-capped tick-gated pin nudge (2026-10-21, owner go after the whip revert — "maybe make a bit more seperation on parry … as natural and smooth transition as possible for the viewer"; sim-data change, fingerprints re-baselined).** `parryPush` 525 → 700 (+33%): a velocity impulse (smooth by construction) that ends grapples before wedges form — fewer pins, fewer nudges — and masks the residual nudge inside the clang's separation motion. The pin-escape nudge's old uncapped one-substep teleport (≤0.6 rad, ~36px of mace head in one drawn frame) is replaced by per-tick-gated adds capped at `nudgePx` 12px-at-the-head (nz ≤ 12/REACH): each escape spreads over ~3–12 ticks at ≤6px-head per drawn frame; adds happen only on stalled substeps — no pending ledger, no drain, so the reverted whip's feed-loop cannot recur. Verified with the duration metric the whip miss taught (20 naturals): tick-entry overlaps 0.12% (502 ticks), **visible (>2.5px) 0**, worst depth **0.81px**, max consecutive overlap run **27 ticks**, glued battles (run >30) **0/20**; snap trace worst per-tick angle add 0.13 rad on the shortest weapons (= their 12px cap), mace-class 0.05 rad — the one-frame warp class is gone. **Flail promotion + per-segment bake (2026-10-21, owner go).** Owner playtest of the delivered art preview, verbatim: "there are no parry and no damage done by the flail, also look at the screenshot, speedy is only shooting arrows in that direction the whole game. the flail looks good now, maybe you should implement the fixes in our spinclash preview instead of the flail 1.html ?" — the first two symptoms are the unbaked-ghost rule (no baked circles = no contacts) and the third is its consequence: a once-per-revolution launcher releases at the same spin phase every revolution, so with no parries to flip the spin (the ghost flail cannot parry) every arrow leaves parallel — growth was verified live and correct (+1.0 spin per landing, 8/8 logged hits). Decision: promote the delivery to canonical and bake. Bake shape: four temp regens (haft+collar+swivel / ring link / bar link / spiked ball+boss), 12+14+15+8 = 49 circles merged into one row; boot containment tagging assigns each circle its segment (innermost wins), `bladeCircles` places them on the articulated pose. Gate: flail poke −0.29px, miss 2.80px — PASS. Fingerprint delta vs `.qa/fp-pre-flail.json`: hammer+dagger s5 12601→12601, staff+hammer s1 9325→9325, bow+crossbow s2 18000→18000, boomerang+shuriken s1 12273→12273, wand+bow s3 18000→18000, shield+hammer s4 12070→12070, natural s7 18000→18000 — all tick-identical (hashes differ only by the inert `fl` field: `newFighter` now carries the chain-state object on every fighter, and the delivered file's diff against the old canonical contains zero non-flail lines); natural s11 18000→17214 = the dice pool growing 13→14 re-rolling that seed into a terminating fight. `.qa/fp-after.json` re-baselined to the post state. Fast tier all green post-bake (chromecheck, killstill, hitstill, reveal-probe, projectile-fix: A 0, B 0/6665, C 0/243965, D 4/18 recorded red). Harness gap disclosed above.

**Flail segment tie v2 — the phantom straight chain (2026-10-22, owner go).** Owner saw it in hitbox mode (seed 52071 screenshot): the gold debug dots floated along a straight line while the drawn chain hung bent — and because `bladeCircles` reads the same `c.s` ties, the *live hitbox* was misplaced too (a visible-contact-first violation, the exact class the inscribe gate exists to kill). Diagnosis (`scratch/segcheck.mjs`): the v1 boot tie demanded each whole circle (centre + 12 rim samples at 0.97r) fit inside one segment's SOLID ink — impossible for the two open-ring links (their holes are ink-free by paint) and for most of the spiked ball, so **46/49 circles silently fell back to segment 0 (haft)**; only 3 rode the ball, none rode the links. Straight/rest pose hid it (the gate passed: poke −0.29px, miss 2.80px); any bend misplaced the hitbox. Fix (tie v2, generic for chain weapons): a circle rides the segment whose OUTER ink (max-area ring per shape — a hole is still that segment's ink) contains its CENTRE, innermost segment first = paint order = what the viewer sees on top; a centre on no ink ties to the nearest segment-ink boundary; **more than `TIE_TOL` 6px from every segment ink = bake/art desync = loud boot throw** — never a silent wrong tie again.
New tally **9/13/14/13** (tie is geometry, not bake-batch order; ball-core circles that also sit under a spike correctly ride the ball), worst fallback distance **0px** (all 49 centres on ink). Proof: (a) bent-pose audit — at 4 forced articulations (straight/bend30/zigzag/full-wrap) **0/49** circle centres off their tied segment's transformed ink, while the v1 counterfactual puts **27/49** dots off the drawn chain in the zigzag pose; (b) attribution — reconstructed the v1 tie byte-for-byte (`scratch/pre-tie-v1.html`), ran natural s7 in lockstep: draft **flail vs flail**, first divergence **tick 386** = a v1-only 5-dmg hit fired while the attacker's stored chain pose `aFl` shows link 1 bent ~30° off spine (the phantom straight chain "touching" across visible empty space); v2 fires nothing there; (c) no under-hitting — forced cells v1→v2: mirrors 23/20/32/28 → 23/29/28/31 dmg-hits, flail-vs-sword 25→27, flail-vs-hammer 29→32 (v2 terminates K1 at t5908), parry counts DROP (16→10, 18→10, 19→16) because the phantom was also parrying things the drawn chain never touched. Both probes' Part A recomputed the contact gap as `bladeCircles(ax, ay, aAng, aW, circ)` — no 6th chain-pose argument — so articulated chain contacts recomputed against a phantom STRAIGHT chain while the sim detected them on the true bent pose.
The renderer was never wrong: the still latch stores `fl: e.aFl` and the view override writes `view[i].fl` → `drawWeaponOf(…, view.fl)` — the stills show the true touch; only the audit didn't know. The class dates to **tie v2** (2026-10-22): before it, the sim's own chain hitbox WAS phantom-straight, so the blind recompute agreed; tie v2's suite did not re-run the stills, and batch-4's 18-weapon re-roll pulled deeply articulated chain hits into the probes' seed sets for the first time. **Fix (harness-only; canonical untouched):** both probes gained the chain-pose argument — `e.aFl` on the stored-contact recompute, `f.fl.u` on the tick-pose recomputes (hitstill's invisible classifier, killstill's gEnd) — with code comments citing the renderer consumer. **Re-run: killstill 31/31 gContact ≤ 0.3 (31 kills — blade 23 / stone 8 — across 14 seeds; stone victim drift worst 0.00px) · hitstill 515/515 gContact ≤ 0.3 (515 non-kill hits — blade 301 / stone 214 — across 15 seeds; the invisible graze class 1/301; stone landings frozen 214/214) · Part B latch checks ok on both.** Same family as the disclosed measure.mjs chain-pose law-audit gap:
the still probes now carry the chain channel; the law audit still lacks it (balance-pass flag stands). **Harness:** `SOLIDITY_CELLS` 12→18 — adds flail+hammer (fixing the flail merge's missed roster update), nunchaku+nunchaku, scythe+scythe, nunchaku+scythe, wand+crossbow (the true worst-speed shot pair, spark 800 + bolt 860), knives+shuriken; 94 battles, ≈9 min at 2 sandbox jobs estimated (re-measure at the next sanctioned run) — and measure.mjs's header comments reconciled to the 18-weapon reality (324-cell matrix ≈2,600 battles ≈2h sequential). `measure.mjs` NOT run (owner-go rule).
