None blocks the bake; all are art/taste material.

**Edge-leading mirror — katana + scythe (2026-10-22, owner observation and decision: "most weapon looks the same spinning left and right, which is fine, but i just noticed katana, since it has a sharp side on its blade, the weapon appears to spin backwards … i was thinking if we could flip the weapon when its turn, to prevent this, and also for future weapons"; built as the opt-in per-weapon `lead` trait after the proposal consultation — owner: "go". Sim-data change, fingerprints re-baselined).** Directionally asymmetric art declares `lead` = the spine-local side bearing its business contour (katana −1 — its KP strip runs the blade's −l boundary; scythe SHIPPED +1 — the edge is the hook's concave face — and was flipped to −1 at the owner's same-day playtest, see the amendment below); the placement layer mirrors art AND hitbox together on spin sign (l → l·flip), so the contour always leads the motion — law 6 guarantees one fighter per round spins each way, so without the mirror one of the two blades always travelled "backwards". Kinematic ground (proven at birth, 64 angles × both signs):
`wAngle += spin·dt` makes the local **+l side lead when spin > 0**. **Mechanism, one source:** `leadFlip(wId, spin)` beside `spineFrame`, exported on `geom` — consumed by the sim's collision (parryGap pose objects gained `fp`; bladeVsBody, the resolver's D0/D1 and the swat check pass `leadFlip(w, spin)` from the live substep spin), the renderer (`polyPath`/`drawWeapon`/`drawWeaponOn`/`drawWeaponOf` carry the factor; `view[i].flip` is binary from the LATEST tick's spin — never blended; the pop-in centre POPC mirrors with the art), the stills (latch stores `fp: leadFlip(e.aW, e.aSp)`, the view override consumes it) and every harness recompute. **Events carry the flip truth:** conditional `aSp` on hits, `aSp`/`bSp` on parries (ENTRY signed spins, captured pre-resolver/pre-flip beside the entry angles — the audit recomputes the mirror the blades had when they touched, not the recoil's), `fSp` on swats — flagged weapons ONLY, so unflagged event JSON stays byte-identical and the delta stays surgical. **Boot guards:** `lead` must be ±1 (loud throw); **chain weapons are forbidden the flag** (mirror × tie/articulation unproven until a real case — loud throw).
Radial-asymmetric art (bow/crossbow bellies, knives' outward blades) must never declare it — mirrored it faces inward, wrong at both signs; the VS poster draws canonical (`drawFitWeapon` takes no flip). **Disclosed artifacts:** (1) the parry pop — the mirror re-presents instantaneously at every clang, consistent with the recoil's own instantaneous snap and masked by the 700 shove (owner-playtest verdict pending; the softer animated-flip alternative was rejected at proposal: during a tween the art would not match the hitbox); (2) on a parry tick the interpolated draw blends angles under the NEW flip — a one-frame class, the same family as the interpolation-base snaps at teleports. REACH/MINR/substep budgets are mirror-invariant (a lateral mirror preserves radii and |offsets|) — the budget law's numbers are untouched; law 6s launch recomputes are flip-invariant (launch points are on-axis, l = 0, or body-radial offsets). **Birth proof `.qa/leadproof.mjs` (permanent — extend PROBES/CELLS for every future `lead` weapon):** Part A — the edge faces the motion at 256/256 angle×sign samples per weapon (criterion: the cross-blade vector P_edge − Q_opposite, probe pairs quoted from each weapon's KP strip vs its opposite contour, maps through the same l·flip and must agree with the motion side); Part A2 — art == hitbox under the mirror:
every point the real `drawWeaponOn` logs through a recording ctx satisfies the circles' exact world mapping at fp = ±1 (katana 78/78, scythe 84/84 both factors); Part B — flagged battles stay auditable from stored fields alone (5 forced cells: 152 flagged hits recompute gContact ≤ 0.3, 188 flagged parry sides to entry gap ≤ 0, 1 flagged swat to touch ≤ 0). **Delta proof (`.qa/fp-pre-lead.json` → `.qa/fp-post-lead.json`): 7/8 cells byte-identical** — all six forced cells plus natural s7 carry no katana/scythe; only natural s11 moved (8556 → 10285; round 1 is mace+scythe — chaotic divergence, still terminating far under cap): the crossbow-rescale proof shape. `.qa/fp-after.json` re-baselined := post. **Suite:** chromecheck PASS · inscription gate PASS with every number UNCHANGED (the flip is a world-placement concern; the bake is spine-local) · killstill 31/31 gContact ≤ 0.3 (stone drift 0.00) · hitstill 542/542 (invisible graze class 5/306 — the s11 re-roll moved the sample; it is the class the still covers) · reveal-probe ALL PASS, zero edits · projectile-fix PROBE PASS (A 0 [non-vacuity 108/614/1304] · B 0/6665 · C 0/243965 · D 4/18 recorded red — figures untouched: its cells carry no flagged weapon) · `scratch/gauntlet-batch4.mjs` re-run:
unflagged cells byte-stable against the batch-4 record, flagged cells diverged chaotically but all contact and all terminate (scythe mirror 64.1→81.8s 49 hits, katana+scythe 66.9s, katana+dagger 142.7s, scythe+mace 118.7s), the two informational knives-mirror caps unchanged. **Harnesses edited in the same pass — the chain-blind lesson applied preemptively:** `.qa/hitstill.mjs`/`.qa/killstill.mjs` recomputes pass `geom.leadFlip` from the stored (`e.aSp`) or live (`f.spin`) sign; `measure.mjs`'s six recompute sites (law 1 stored-pose touch, law 2 entry-pose gap, 2s swat touch, the law-4 stuck-flag replica, the 3s foe-blade circles, and `gapAt`) derive the flip from the events' spin fields or the auditSubs records' own signed spins (`A_SPIN`/`B_SPIN` — no new audit channel needed). **`measure.mjs` edited, NOT run (owner-go rule)** — the full law re-proof rides with the next sanctioned run. The disclosed chain-pose law-audit gap is unchanged (flagged weapons are rigid-only by the boot guard). Doctrine for future weapons is prescriptive in **Weapon art** (5). **Playtest amendment — the scythe's lead flipped +1 → −1 (same day, owner verdict: "its perfect on katana, but scythe is spinning the wrong way from the beginning, so it will always be backwards").** The shipped +1 presented the hook's mouth/edge strip toward the motion — the same edge-forward criterion the katana approved; the owner's eye reads a mouth-forward hook as the weapon rotating backwards.
For HOOKS the sanctioned reading is **beak-forward**: the point stabs the direction of travel, the mouth scoops behind (the kusarigama-spin idiom). Root cause of the miss: the birth proof's scythe probe measured the edge strip's side on a constant-d cut — degenerate for a hook whose edge faces −d (its mouth opens toward the grip), so it proved a criterion that was not the visible reading; the geometry passed and the art-class criterion was wrong. The lesson, recorded: for a new art class the probe must encode the VISIBLE reading, and the owner's playtest remains the criterion that outranks both (the house loop exactly as designed — he reports the symptom, the fix ships with a corrected proof). Changes: scythe `lead` −1 (its beak sits at −l in the canonical art, so the flip now presents the beak to the motion at both signs); `.qa/leadproof.mjs` Part A's scythe probe replaced with the beak-vs-spine-axis pair ([80,−75] vs [80,0]) and its doctrine comments corrected to the business-contour language; the katana untouched (owner: "perfect"). Sim-data change — the day's second lead ritual:
`.qa/fp-pre-leadfix.json` → `.qa/fp-post-leadfix.json`: **7/8 cells byte-identical again**, only natural s11 moved (10285 → 8820; the mace+scythe round, chaotic divergence, terminates 73.5s); `.qa/fp-after.json` re-baselined := post. Suite re-run: leadproof ALL PASS (beak faces motion 256/256, art==hitbox mapping 84/84 at both factors, Part B 168 flagged hits / 224 parry sides / 1 swat recomputed from stored fields) · chromecheck PASS · inscription gate PASS, numbers unchanged (local space) · killstill 30/30 · hitstill 525/525 · reveal-probe ALL PASS, zero edits · projectile-fix PROBE PASS (A/B/C figures untouched, D 4/18 recorded) · gauntlet-batch4: flagged cells diverged, every one contacts and terminates (scythe mirror 61.9s, scythe+mace 90.7s, katana+scythe 70.4s); unflagged cells byte-stable against the batch-4 record. `measure.mjs` needed NO edit (it derives the flip from the data's `lead` via `geom.leadFlip`) and was NOT run (owner-go rule). (The re-presentation glint — the first snap-softening attempt, owner option 2, iterated twice (invisible, then disliked) and REMOVED the same day in favour of the flip transition below — is retired verbatim to CHRONICLE-archive's round-4 section; its permanent outcomes are the **build stamp** convention and the recorded FX-contrast lesson.) **Flip transition — the glint REMOVED (same day, owner verdict after glint r2: "its working now, but visually i dont like it, i thought something else, like a fast transition not an instant snap").** The mirror flip is now animated:
the flagged weapon's DISPLAYED lateral factor eases old → edge-on → mirrored as cos(π·p) over `CFG.flipMs` (90 dial) — the blade reads as spinning about its own spine, the "fast transition" the owner asked for from the start, built as his informed pick after the option-3 trade was presented twice (the proposal menu, then the glint-r2 report). **The disclosed trade (owner-accepted):** for the ~90ms window after a flagged parry the DRAWN art is between two legal poses while the sim hitbox is already at the new (true) pose — the drawn≠hitbox class the original proposal rejected. Containment, each one proven: (1) damage events inside the window freeze into their FULL true contact poses — the stills latch the binary ±1, so every moment that matters is shown honest (killstill 30/30, hitstill 525/525 unchanged); (2) the hitbox debug overlay draws sim truth only (`view[i].fpSim`, never the squash) — the owner's inspection mode never lies; (3) the sim/laws/audits never see it — `leadFlip` stays binary, events untouched, **fingerprints 8/8 byte-identical** (renderer-only proven, no re-ritual, baseline untouched); (4) the flutter gate (16 ticks ≈ 133ms > flipMs) + the 700 separation shove make re-contact inside the window structurally rare — the residual class is a non-frozen parry/swat frame within ~5 drawn frames of a flagged clang; (5) the transition is FIGHT-PHASE-ONLY — every phase entry snaps (the bell's pop-in materialises blades at true orientation; a roll is not a parry — the birth run's T3 FAIL caught the reveal/bell firing and the phase-entry snap cured it), it freezes with the frame under a hitstop (the dtFrames clock), and resetFx snaps it at round rolls.
Generic for any `lead` weapon at the owner's standing reason ("at some point we are probably gonan have similar weapons"). **The glint was removed wholesale** (CFG line, state, the consumeEvents parry branch, drawGlint, the draw-loop line, the decay term, the info field) — punctuation for a snap that no longer snaps; owner: "visually i dont like it". `.qa/glintproof.mjs` retired with it (the glint paragraphs above are its permanent record); **`.qa/flipproof.mjs`** is the new standing proof: T1 shape (11 episodes over the flagged battle, each through near edge-on |cur| ≤ 0.3, landing EXACTLY ±1, 11/11), T2 the unflagged side and the all-unflagged control never animate (sim-scan non-vacuity both directions: 28 parries / 11 flagged-side vs 15 / 0), T3 the bell snap (no animation before the first flagged parry), T4 the window bounded (max 5 frames ≤ budget 8 at 90ms). Suite: flipproof ALL PASS · chromecheck PASS · leadproof ALL PASS (the ±1 passthrough is exact — the continuous factor exists only renderer-side) · killstill 30/30 · hitstill 525/525 · reveal-probe ALL PASS zero edits · inscription gate PASS · projectile-fix PROBE PASS (A 0 [108/614/1304] · B 0/6665 · C 0/243965 · D 4/18 — the baseline figures exactly).

Dial for the owner's eye: `CFG.flipMs` (90 — lower is snappier, higher is smoother; the containment window moves with it). **Dial bump (same day, owner playtest: "its still too snappy"): `CFG.flipMs` 90 → 180, stamp flip-r4.** Because 180ms now outlasts the flutter gate (16 ticks ≈ 133ms), the sweep formula stores its start side (`from`) instead of reading the live target: a mid-sweep re-parry LANDS the first flip and the at-rest check starts a second continuous one — two clangs read as two flips, never a mid-air jump. **Javelin design lock + shot-kind pre-wire + rider brief (2026-10-22, owner: "i wanna start with javelin and it should be thrown" → "i agree, tangential and also with the throw direction, dont bother about skills stats/heavy profile, etc for now").** Design locked: launcher, `hold: 'tangential'`, `tuck: false`, `len: LT` — the longest rim chord on the roster — **released tangentially in the spin direction** (the boomerang/shuriken family: the stone config's missing `fire`/`off` fields ARE the release rule; the thrown copy leaves along the held spear's own axis). The radial-hold alternative lost on the silhouette collision with the melee spear (owner took the tangential fork after the read). Straight flight, dies on the rim, swattable: **zero new law surface** (6s already recomputes tangential-spin releases, the 5s budget just sees one more speed, 3s/4s untouched). Stats/profile deferred to the balance pass at owner word — the row ships class-default and provisional: ω 3.5 / dmg 5 / `+0.7 SPD` · stone `{ r: 7, speed: 540, len: 46, kind: 'javelin', what: 'javelin' }` · line `'a spear per rev · blades swat javelins'`.
No `lead`: the brief mandates bilateral symmetry about the spine (a mirror across the spine cannot re-point a head that lies ALONG it; both ends deliberate — tail ferrule sanctioned — because which end faces the motion swaps with the spin). **Pre-wire (renderer-only, inert — the nunchaku `ch.np` pattern):** `drawShot` gained the `kind === 'javelin'` early-out (`drawArrow` in `KW` shaft + `KS` head — the knives-borrowed-bolt-art flag not repeated: deliveries preview complete, zero renderer edits at reconcile) and `HALO.javelin` (steel-family 0.18, the bolt/arrow value — the every-kind-inks-its-hitbox directive covered from day one); both unreachable until a WEAPONS row emits the kind. Proof: fingerprints **8/8 byte-identical** · chromecheck PASS · inscription gate PASS (18 rows unchanged). Disclosed self-catch: the first HALO paste accidentally altered the kindless `arrow` entry — spotted and restored in the same pass, before any proof ran. **`javelin-artist-brief.md` at the repo root, paste-ready against current canonical** — the documented rider structure rebuilt from the file (code-beats-comments):
OUTPUT RULES (complete-file / never-images / never-ask / three zones only) · BASE FILE TRUTH with STALE-BASE markers (`CFG.flipMs` present + `WEAPON_IDS` ending `'knives'`; a stale base is deliver-and-note, non-fatal) and the `|| []` ghost rule as the EXPECTED delivery state (no circles authored, ever — the gate's `n=0 MISS-FAIL` on the javelin row is the DoD, not a defect) · the prescriptive handheld spec quoted from file (LT = `maxD(INGR_MACE)` ≈ 113.4 pre-scale, six palette hexes, ink `#2e2b22` 3px counted as body by the gate, ~11.5u plain shaft, flat plain butt + sanctioned tail ferrule) · the design sheet (grip binding at mid-d on the rim, ends ≈21.5u clear of the bodyR-64 disc at LT, KS leaf head with KP edge sliver over the outer 15–20%, ≥4u feature floor with the disclosed tip taper, squint-read apart from the melee spear and bow) · the verbatim WEAPONS row (data, not design) · exactly three sanctioned zones with file anchors (after the INGR_KNIVES block's closing `[KG, rect(3, 75, -19.5, -15)]]);` · after the knives row · WEAPON_IDS append) · mechanics invariants · ghost-bake definition of done (boots clean, pool 19, poster + legend render, watermark shows, **zero non-javelin diff lines**).
The general and nunchaku briefs remain on the owner's side; this rider is standalone. At reconcile: the usual pipeline (bake → gate → gauntlet → ritual → docs → owner playtest) plus the roster-change duties (`SOLIDITY_CELLS`, the ingest gauntlet count, matrix-cell counts 19² = 361).

**Javelin delivered and reconciled (2026-10-22, owner-uploaded `spinclash-preview javelin.html`; sim-data change — new weapon — fingerprints re-baselined).** The first single-weapon rider commission, delivered against CURRENT canonical (STALE-BASE markers checked and noted in the artist's header `observed:` line, including the sharp catch that `INGR_JAVELIN` sits before the `LT` declaration — the art uses the literal tip 113.44 ≤ LT = 113.4419, so no TDZ, and the WEAPONS row's `len: LT` evaluates after it). **Delivery shape:** six diff hunks — the three sanctioned zones (INGR_JAVELIN 7-shape block: KG tail ferrule d0-6, KW shaft d6-67 at the 11.5u doctrine, KD grip binding d51-62.4 marking the rim grip at LT/2, KG socket collar d64-68, slim KS leaf head d68-113.44 at ~40% of len with a KP bevel sliver flagged paint-only; the verbatim WEAPONS row; WEAPON_IDS append → pool 19) plus a stray leading CR from transfer (normalized at promotion) plus **ONE owner-directed renderer edit — the first artist-side renderer touch, sanctioned in-session**: the pre-wired `drawArrow(KW, KS)` flight call replaced by a dedicated `drawJavelin` (bare wood shaft, gold tail ferrule, big steel leaf head with KP bevel, no fletching — owner word via the artist's comment: "javelins fly as javelins, not arrows").
Reviewed at reconcile: palette-clean (KW/KG/KS/KP + botInk), house drawArrow idioms (velocity-aligned, save/restore, lineWidth 2), the halo branch untouched so the hitbox disc still inks itself; the leaf head leads its hitbox disc by ~2.5 disc-radii — the arrows'/bolts' disclosed safe-direction class (ink leads the hit, never a gap-hit), bigger by design. Art is bilaterally symmetric about the spine, no `lead` (a mirror cannot re-point a head that lies along d; both ends deliberate per the brief — ferrule tail). **Bake:** trimmed-`WEAPON_IDS` temp → `--regen`: **19 circles, minR 2.80 — the roster's HIGHEST (a slim, stout-enough silhouette: zero substep-budget cost), poke −0.30, miss 2.46** — no floor exception needed (contrast the scythe's beak). Gate PASS all 19; chains unchanged (flail 9/13/14/13 0/49 ×3, nunchaku 8/19 0/27 ×3). **Promotion:** canonical := delivery + the baked row + CR normalization + stamp `build javelin-r5`; the artist's verbatim original stays in `deliveries/`. **Ritual:** `.qa/fp-pre-javelin.json` → `.qa/fp-post-javelin.json`: **6 forced cells byte-identical** (no javelin in any; no new fighter/event fields — the stone config adds no schema), both naturals re-rolled by the pool growth 18→19:
s7 → javelin+javelin (holds the 18000 cap — the mirror-length red class explains it), s11 8820→8888 (mace+katana/bow+spear, Heavy 74.1s). `.qa/fp-after.json` re-baselined := post. **Gauntlet** (`scratch/gauntlet-javelin.mjs`, kept as the birth record): every cell contacts; **the javelin's spine dealt 0 blade hits across all cells (projectile rule holds)**; swats live (6 vs hammer, 12 vs shuriken, 24 vs crossbow); terminates vs hammer 98.4s / vs scythe 69.2s / vs shuriken 111.8s; THREE informational caps — javelin mirror, javelin+crossbow, javelin+bow — the pure-projectile long-cell red class the javelin now belongs to (open question 1; its 52-throw/1-landing rate vs hammer says the r7/540 profile is accuracy-poor at these speeds — balance-pass material, recorded not judged); maxSpin 23.5 on javelin+bow (under the 25 buzzsaw line). **Suite:** chromecheck PASS · killstill 28/28 · hitstill 457/457 (drifted counts = the re-rolled naturals; the class is unchanged) · reveal-probe ALL PASS, zero edits (the poster draws the new silhouette from its poly) · flipproof ALL PASS · leadproof ALL PASS · projectile-fix PROBE PASS (A 0 [108/614/1304] · B 0/6665 · C 0/243965 · D 4/18 — its fixed cells carry no javelin, figures untouched) · inscription gate PASS.
**Harness:** `SOLIDITY_CELLS` 18→19 (+`javelin+javelin` — longest-chord mirror + the red class it joins; 97 battles ≈10 min at 2 sandbox jobs, re-measure at the next sanctioned run); matrix counts reconciled to 361 cells ≈2,900 battles in measure.mjs's comments and the docs; ingest's gauntlet now faces 18 opponents. `measure.mjs` edited, NOT run (owner-go rule). **Owner playtest is the final gate** — good seeds: s7 (javelin mirror), s2/s3/s9 forced via the seed row, and any natural roll in slow motion to judge the flight art.

**Page-shot pre-wire + the tome rider brief (2026-10-22, immediately after the javelin reconcile per the agreed sequencing — owner design lock from chat: "i was actually thinking making the tome tangential, and then make it look like an open book, with pages (not wide open)").** `kind: 'page'` pre-wired into `drawShot` (a tumbling loose page in the star/spark spin idiom — KP paper, ink outline, one small KG seal; art stays inside the halo'd hitbox disc per the disc-shot rule) + `HALO.page` (paper-family 0.22) — inert until a WEAPONS row emits the kind: fingerprints **8/8 byte-identical** against the fresh javelin baseline, chromecheck PASS, gate PASS. Design decisions as locked in chat: tangential (the owner overrode the assistant's radial recommendation — his call, recorded), the shot = a spinning page (the assistant's pick following his pages direction; the rune-glyph veto window went unclaimed), stats class-default provisional (r 6 / 680 / off 14).

**`headLead` — the javelin's head-flip (2026-10-22, owner playtest of the merged javelin: "i think the javelin should also flip around like scythe and katana"; sim-data change, fingerprints re-baselined).** Symptom confirmed before building (house loop): the tangential kinematics give v·d̂ = bodyR·spin — the head end of the chord travels the way it points only when spin > 0; measured at 64 angles × both signs: spin>0 head-first 64/64, **spin<0 TAIL-first 64/64** — the owner's symptom, one javelin orbiting backwards per round, swapped at every parry, the katana's class on the other axis. The lateral `lead` mirror cannot fix it: it maps (d,l) → (d,−l) and the javelin's art is bilaterally symmetric in l BY BRIEF DESIGN — its asymmetry runs along the chord. **Mechanism — sibling trait, one factor:** `headLead: ±1` (the d-end bearing the head; javelin +1 = the d = len end); `leadFlip` generalised to read `lead ?? headLead` — every consumer (the sim's collision, the renderer's view build, the stills, every harness recompute) inherits through the one source with zero call-site changes; the placement layer d-mirrors about the chord midpoint:
d' = len/2 + (d − len/2)·fp in `bladeCircles` and `polyPath` alike — the grip at t = 0.5 on the rim IS the mirror's fixed point, which is why the trait is tangential-only — with the exact fp === 1 fast path keeping every unflagged weapon byte-identical; the POPC pop-in centre rides the same formula. **Boot guards (loud throws):** headLead ∈ {±1}; `hold === 'tangential'` required (a radial grip at d 0 would slide off the body under the d-mirror); lead XOR headLead (one factor, one axis — the combination is unproven); never on chains. **Events:** the conditional signed-spin fields widened to `lead || headLead` (hit `aSp`, parry ENTRY `aSp`/`bSp`, swat `fSp`) — the audits and stills derive the mirror through the same `geom.leadFlip`; unflagged-cell event JSON stays byte-identical. The launch doctrine is flip-invariant by construction: the middle release point (t = 0.5) is the pivot itself (6s untouched). **The transition:** the same `flipAnim` state machine — the javelin's re-presentation eases through "edge-on" as the chord contracting onto the grip and re-extending reversed (a spear tumbling end-over-end in foreshortened projection), `CFG.flipMs` 180, the disclosed art≠hitbox window and its containment identical to the lead entry (stills latch the full ±1, the debug overlay draws the sim truth `fpSim`, the sim/laws/audits never see it).
VS poster canonical (`drawFitWeapon` takes no factor). **Proofs:** `.qa/leadproof.mjs` extended — Part A axis-aware probes (the javelin criterion: the head-tip-vs-grip chord offset faces the motion side, 256/256 angle×sign samples; katana/scythe unchanged), Part A2's mapping replay generalised to the d-axis rule (javelin 72/72 drawn points at both factors, bit-exact against polyPath's own formula), Part B + 2 cells (javelin+javelin s7 — parries carrying `aSp`/`bSp` through the d-mirror; javelin+bow s5 — the flagged swat, `fSp`): **ALL PASS** (168 flagged blade hits, 224 flagged parry sides, 1 flagged swat, every one recomputed from stored fields alone). `.qa/flipproof.mjs`'s control seed moved s7 → s1 (s7 now rolls the flagged javelin mirror; s1 = spear/boomerang + flail/mace, 12 unflagged parries): ALL PASS (9 episodes, exact landings, control dark). Diagnosis anecdote, recorded because the house trap bit again: the first symptom probe ALIASED `spineFrame`'s shared `_fr` scratch (two calls, one object — "read before recall", the canonical's own comment says so) and reported tail-first at BOTH signs; the corrected read gave the true 64/64 split.
**Delta proof** (`.qa/fp-pre-headlead.json` → `.qa/fp-post-headlead.json`): **7/8 cells byte-identical** — all six forced cells AND natural s11 (mace+katana/bow+spear: the katana's lead mechanics untouched, no javelin in its rolls); only natural s7 moved (the javelin mirror — hash drifts, holds the cap; its weapon is the one whose geometry changed). `.qa/fp-after.json` re-baselined := post. **Gauntlet** (`scratch/gauntlet-javelin.mjs` re-run): every cell contacts, the spine still deals 0 blade hits (projectile rule holds), terminations shifted inside their class (vs hammer 97.4s, vs scythe 76.2s, vs shuriken 145.5s), the three launcher caps unchanged (mirror-length red class), maxSpin ≤ 23.5. **Suite:** chromecheck PASS · killstill 29/29 · hitstill 473/473 (drifted counts = re-rolled dynamics, class unchanged) · reveal-probe ALL PASS, zero edits · projectile-fix PROBE PASS (A 0 [108/614/1304] · B 0/6665 · C 0/243965 · D 4/18 — fixed cells, figures untouched) · inscription gate PASS (numbers unchanged — local space). `measure.mjs` needed NO edit (its six recompute sites already call `geom.leadFlip`, now generalised — the one-source design paying its rent) and was NOT run (owner-go rule). Stamp: **build headlead-r6**. Owner playtest is the final gate — s7 is a javelin mirror: both spears head-first at all times, re-presenting through the 180ms tumble at every clang.

**Tome delivered and reconciled (2026-10-22, owner-uploaded `spinclash-preview tome.html`; sim-data change — new weapon — fingerprints re-baselined).** The second single-rider commission and the first to consume a shot-kind pre-wire exactly as designed — `kind: 'page'` (pre-wired inert at the brief's birth) flew the moment the WEAPONS row existed: **zero renderer lines in the delivery**, the knives-borrowed-art flag structurally impossible. **Delivery shape:** the three sanctioned zones + the recurring stray leading CR (normalized at promotion): `INGR_TOME` (18 shapes: KD cover band — a shallow tent, centreline slope 0.10, ends d 4/74; eight KP page leaves = four nested layers × left/right with half-spans 32/29/26/23 and fore-edges protruding 2.5/1.7/0.9/0.1u past the cover in a quarter-sine droop — the owner's "open book, not wide open" in polygon form; four vis-flagged KD text lines — paint INSIDE the page union, zero covering impact; KW spine block d 33-45 at the gutter (the grip, chord middle on the rim) bound by a KG strap with a vis-flagged KB gem; KG end bands d 3-8/70-75 — both visible ends deliberate per the tangential rule), the WEAPONS row, and the WEAPON_IDS append → pool 20.
**The `observed:` discipline set a new bar:** markers all three checked; the sanctioned `off` tune **14 → 10 with its reason** (the fore-edge reaches l ≈ −14.3 at the chord ends but only ≈ −9 beside the gutter where the page actually leaves — the launch point sits at the mouth, not past it); KP-as-PAPER logged as the owner's named extension; the |l| extent ≈ +6…−14.3 disclosed against the ~18u guide (the spine block's +6 bulge keeps the grip reading as a binding); page-tip tapers pre-declared as expected miss band; ghost state and gate FAIL anticipated verbatim. No `lead`/`headLead`: pages-outward is radial asymmetry (belly class) and the silhouette is bilateral about mid-d — the taxonomy's third case, correctly declared as nothing. **Bake:** trimmed-`WEAPON_IDS` temp → `--regen`: **13 circles, minR 2.90 — the new roster highest** (a compact, stout silhouette: zero substep-budget cost), poke −0.31, miss 2.53 — no floor exception. Gate PASS all 20; chains unchanged. **Promotion:** canonical := delivery + baked row + CR normalization + stamp `build tome-r7`; the verbatim original stays in `deliveries/`.
**Ritual:** `.qa/fp-pre-tome.json` → `.qa/fp-post-tome.json`: **6 forced cells byte-identical**; both naturals re-rolled by the pool growth 19→20 — s7 → tome+tome/bow+crossbow (holds the cap, hash drifts) and **s11 8888 → 18000 (new cap) — diagnosed before acceptance per the REACH-fix precedent: s11 re-rolled into [shield, knives] round 1, the recorded shield-cannot-damage × projectile-length double red (the knives side cannot reach the shield fighter fast enough inside 150s) — divergence into a KNOWN class, not tome pathology**. `.qa/fp-after.json` re-baselined := post. **Gauntlet** (`scratch/gauntlet-tome.mjs`, kept as the birth record — it carries the launch-doctrine recompute for the tuned off): every cell contacts; **the tome's spine dealt 0 blade hits (projectile rule holds)**; **launch doctrine 827/827 on the middle + off·radial point, worst 0.00e+0px** — the artist's off-10 tune lands every page exactly at the mouth; swats live (27 vs shuriken); terminations: vs hammer 85.1s, vs shuriken 140.4s, vs scythe 64.2s; three informational caps (tome mirror, tome+bow, tome+javelin — the projectile-mirror/duo red class; landing rates healthy: 7/59 vs hammer against the javelin's 1/52 — balance-pass material).
**Probe maintenance at this merge (recorded so the next merge expects it):** `.qa/flipproof.mjs` broke in two instructive ways — (1) its sim-scan detected flags via `.lead` only, blind to the day-old `headLead` (the javelin was invisible to it: side −1, NaN cascade, zero episodes); (2) BOTH its seeds churned — pool growth re-rolled s11 off flagged weapons and s1 ONTO them (control animated: min |cur| 0.113, a real transition caught by a probe whose premise had expired). Fixes: the scan checks either trait; the flag seed moved to **s2** (flail+javelin — the flagged side now exercises the d-axis headLead tumble, 11 episodes, all edge-on, all landing exact); the control moved to **s4** (crossbow+wand, 14 parries) and Part A gained the **`rollsFlagged` tripwire** — the control's ROLLS are now asserted flagged-free, so the next roster growth fails loudly at seed selection instead of silently invalidating T2. `.qa/leadproof.mjs` untouched (tome declares no trait). **Suite:** chromecheck PASS · killstill 27/27 · hitstill 467/467 (drifted counts = the re-rolled naturals, class unchanged) · reveal-probe ALL PASS, zero edits · flipproof ALL PASS (post-fix) · leadproof ALL PASS · projectile-fix PROBE PASS (A 0 [108/614/1304] · B 0/6665 · C 0/243965 · D 4/18 — fixed cells, figures untouched) · inscription gate PASS.

**Scroll delivered and reconciled (2026-10-23, owner-uploaded `spinclash-preview scroll.html`; sim-data change — new weapon — fingerprints re-baselined).** The third single-rider commission. **Delivery shape:** the recurring stray leading CR (normalized at promotion) + the three sanctioned zones — zero sim lines, zero renderer lines, all three STALE-BASE markers checked in the `observed:` record. **The owner's in-session art direction (nine redesign rounds, all disclosed in the artist's header — the javelin drawJavelin class of sanctioned deviation, art-side):** (1) **four off-palette fills declared IN-ZONE** — `KT #ecd5a3` parchment, `KTS #c9a35a` layer band, `KR #b8352c` ribbon, `KRD #8f2a24` ribbon shade, the owner's reference colours for the "ancient scroll" look; the six shared palette consts untouched — **the first per-weapon palette extension on the roster, and a ratification item for the convention close**; (2) **`len` 88 — above the rider's 55–78 band at the owner's word ("a bit bigger")**, still ≪ LT ≈ 113.4, implemented as a disclosed post-build uniform scale loop inside the zone (coordinates authored at 76, ×88/76 about the spine origin — mathematically identical to authoring at 88; the bake re-derives circles from the scaled polys, so the inscription regime sees the truth); (3) torn aged edges = body-outline notches ≤1.3u deep (none under the ribbon) + an open seam as vis paint; (4) a KW spindle showing as knobbed handles (KG octagons) past KG disc caps at both chord ends; (5) the slanted KR ribbon at the grip centroid (d 38 authored → 44 = len/2 scaled — the rim grip), slant = paint-level asymmetry, correctly NO lead/headLead.
Art inventory: KW spindle d1-75 · KT barrel d12-64 |l|6 torn · KTS layer bands ×2 (vis) · KD seams/creases ×4 (vis — interior paint, zero covering impact) · KG caps ×2 + knobs ×2 · KR ribbon + KRD shade (vis). **Row:** `len: 88`, stone r 6 / 660 / `off` tuned 10 → 11 (cap/ribbon surface ≈8.7 world + clearance — the sanctioned tune with its reason; the header carries both off values from the redesign layers, the ROW is the truth), kind 'scroll' (the pre-wire consumed with zero renderer edits, the page pattern). **Bake:** trimmed temp → `--regen`: **25 circles, minR 2.40, poke −0.30, miss 2.28** — the torn notches cost nothing (outline wobble, not hairline paint); gate PASS all 21; chains unchanged (flail 9/13/14/13 0/49 ×3, nunchaku 8/19 0/27 ×3). **The reconcile's ONE edit — the flight-art retint (renderer-only, disclosed, owner-vetoable):** the artist deferred the tint ("the flight shot stays the pre-wired KP roll — implementer may retint it"); the `drawShot` scroll branch and `HALO.scroll` now match the held art's owner-reference colours (KT parchment roll, KR ribbon, KG knobs; halo → KT family 0.24) so the held scroll and the flying scroll are the same object.
Retint fingerprint identity proven separately: 8/8. **Promotion:** canonical := delivery + baked row + CR normalization + retint + stamp `build scroll-r8`; the verbatim original stays in `deliveries/`. **Ritual:** `.qa/fp-pre-scroll.json` → `.qa/fp-post-scroll.json`: **6 forced cells byte-identical**; both naturals re-rolled at the pool growth 20→21 and both hold the 18000 cap — **diagnosed before acceptance: s7 → scroll+scroll + bow+boomerang (DOUBLE projectile-mirror rounds), s11 → shield+javelin (the shield×launcher red combo, the tome-merge class again)** — known-red divergence, not scroll pathology. `.qa/fp-after.json` re-baselined := post. **Gauntlet** (`scratch/gauntlet-scroll.mjs`, kept as the birth record — carries the launch-doctrine recompute for the tuned off): every cell contacts; the spine dealt 0 blade hits (projectile rule holds); **launch doctrine 867/867 exact at off 11, worst 0.00e+0px**; swats live (29 vs shuriken); terminations vs hammer 127.8s / vs shuriken 127.4s / vs scythe 99.9s; three informational caps (scroll mirror, scroll+tome — the paper duel — and scroll+bow):
**the scroll is the TENTH launcher; the projectile-mirror red family keeps growing and is the rework's biggest agenda item.** **Suite:** chromecheck PASS · killstill 24/24 · hitstill 433/433 · reveal-probe ALL PASS, zero edits · flipproof ALL PASS — **its seeds survived the pool-21 re-roll** (the rollsFlagged tripwire silent: s2 still rolls flagged — 15 episodes, exact landings — and control s4 still clean; the tripwire earned its keep one merge after birth) · leadproof ALL PASS · projectile-fix PROBE PASS (A 0 [108/614/1304] · B 0/6665 · C 0/243965 · D 4/18 — fixed cells, figures untouched) · inscription gate PASS. **Harness:** `SOLIDITY_CELLS` 20→21 (+`scroll+scroll` — the tenth launcher's mirror; 103 battles ≈10 min at 2 sandbox jobs); matrix counts → 441 cells ≈3,500 battles across measure.mjs's comments and the docs; ingest's gauntlet faces 20 opponents. `measure.mjs` edited, NOT run — the sanctioned re-proof stays deferred to the rework window at the owner's word ("we will talk about this once the weapons are done"). **Owner playtest is the final gate** — s7 rolls scroll+scroll; the one taste call to judge is the KT/KR flight retint against the held art.

**Orb delivered and reconciled — ROSTER COMPLETE AT 22 (2026-10-23, owner-uploaded `spinclash-preview orb.html`; sim-data change — new weapon — fingerprints re-baselined).** The queue's last weapon and its leanest delivery: the usual stray CR (normalized) + the three sanctioned zones, zero sim lines, zero renderer lines (nothing to pre-wire — melee, no shot kind), all three STALE-BASE markers checked. **Art = THREE shapes** (the roster minimum): a KB 14-gon sphere via the in-file `star()` helper (rIn = rOut = 16, centre exactly (54, 0) = (len, 0) per the brief), one flat KG rune band across the equator (5.2u wide — bakes), one vis-flagged KP specular triangle. No shaft, cord, glow, trail or motes — the true-sentinel design as revealed and commissioned. **The owner's in-session iteration record (six quoted words in the header):** "too far out, make it closer to the body and just a bit larger" → … → "make it closer" ×3 — `len` 100 → 80 → 72 → 68 → 64 → 60 → **54** (below the brief's 90–110 band) and sphere r 12 → 13 → **16** (above the 11–13 band): bands are guidance, owner taste overrides (the axe2-D haft precedent), and he iterated against the LIVE ghost preview, so the orbit look is empirically his.
**The artist's geometry note was WRONG and is corrected here (code beats comments):** the header claims "the orb now overlaps the disc by ≈15px … raise len to ≈65 for a just-touching look" — that math measured the orbit from the body centre as len·1.3 = 70px, omitting the radial spine origin offset (bodyR·SPINE_R0 = 61.44 pre-scale). True geometry: the orb centre orbits at **131.6 world px** from the body centre, its inner edge at 110.8 against bodyR 64 — a **clean ≈47px float gap, no overlap**, and the "just-touching" suggestion is moot. **`len` semantics (first row whose art extends past its len):** len = the orbit radius to the orb CENTRE; the art reaches d 70 > 54. Every consumer audited at merge: REACH/SPAN read the poly vertices (REACH ≈ 154 world — well inside the hammer's 231), there is no tip launch (melee), no headLead pivot, and the pop-in centre = bbox centre = the orb centre ✓. No lead/headLead (a sphere has no facing — the taxonomy's third case, declared in the header). **Bake:** trimmed temp → `--regen`: **ONE circle, r 21.4, poke −0.31, miss 0.91px** — the leanest and tightest covering on the roster (a 14-gon + stroke union is a disc; one maximal-inscribed circle covers it — the 0.91 miss is the chord faceting) and MINR 21.4 is an order of magnitude above everything else, so orb pairs cost the parry substep budget almost nothing.
Gate PASS all 22; chains unchanged (flail 9/13/14/13 0/49 ×3, nunchaku 8/19 0/27 ×3). **Promotion:** canonical := delivery + baked row + CR normalization + stamp `build orb-r9`; the verbatim original stays in `deliveries/`. **Ritual:** `.qa/fp-pre-orb.json` → `.qa/fp-post-orb.json`: **7/8 byte-identical** — all six forced cells AND natural s11, which survived a pool growth tick-unchanged for the FIRST time (its shield+javelin roll repeated at pool 22); s7 moved 18000 → **13664, ESCAPING its cap**: it re-rolled into orb+orb, and the melee mirror terminates (Heavy wins 113.9s) — the projectile-mirror reds are a projectile problem, not a mirror problem. `.qa/fp-after.json` re-baselined := post. **Gauntlet** (`scratch/gauntlet-orb.mjs`, kept as the birth record): the sentinel BONKS — 30/52/37/25/24 melee hits across orb+hammer (63.9s), orb mirror (130.9s), orb+katana (117.8s), orb+flail (83.5s), orb+shield (**139.0s, TERMINATES — the shield stalemate needs a no-damage opponent; the orb ends it**); orb+bow caps (the mixed long-cell family — 121 thrown / 10 orb swats / 30 parries, informational); naturals:
s7 orb+orb winner Heavy 113.9s (matches the fingerprint), s11 shield+javelin cap (the known shield×launcher red, unchanged). **Suite:** chromecheck PASS · killstill 25/25 · hitstill 451/451 · reveal-probe ALL PASS, zero edits · flipproof ALL PASS — **its seeds survived a SECOND straight pool growth** (the rollsFlagged tripwire silent two merges running; s2 still flagged, s4 control clean) · leadproof ALL PASS · projectile-fix PROBE PASS (A 0 [108/614/1304] · B 0/6665 · C 0/243965 · D 4/18 — fixed cells, figures untouched) · inscription gate PASS. **Harness:** `SOLIDITY_CELLS` 21→23 (+`orb+orb` mirror pins, +`orb+hammer` big-head×big-head — the hammer+axe family's successor; 109 battles ≈11 min at 2 sandbox jobs, slightly over the 10-min target — disclosed, ≈3–4 min on the owner's machine); matrix counts → 484 cells ≈3,900 battles ≈2.5h sequential across measure.mjs's comments and the docs; ingest's gauntlet faces 21 opponents. `measure.mjs` edited, NOT run — the sanctioned re-proof stays deferred to the rework window at the owner's word. **Disclosed for playtest:** the VS poster bbox-fits the shaft-less orb LARGE (its ~34-world-wide art in the 245×150 fit box → ≈4× scale — the brief's accepted default, flagged there so the artist wouldn't "fix" it); the owner's eye judges; if it reads wrong the cure is a per-weapon poster-scale hint (renderer-only).
**Owner playtest is the final gate** — watch: the orbit reversing at every clang, the growth quickening it, the poster, and the ≈47px float gap he dialed in. **ROSTER COMPLETE: 22 weapons LANDED at roster-r12, the commission queue is empty. Refactor and consolidate where appropriate, simplify the implementation, improve readability and maintainability, optimize inefficient code, and fix any issues found without changing the intended behavior. check if the docs align with everything, trim parts and clean up the workspace for files you dont use"; hygiene pass only — the single canonical edit is comment-only, fingerprints proven 8/8 byte-identical to `.qa/fp-after.json` after, baseline NOT moved per the behaviour-free-refactor rule).** **Code review verdict: CLEAN.** Zero removed-mechanism residue (glint state/function/dial greps: 0); every helper and const alive (usage sweep over star/arcBand/maxD/clamp01/revealFrame/PLATE/FITB/fitBounds/drawFitWeapon/SKILL_SHORT/TINTS/lastShots/deathSeq/auditFl/chainFrames/flail family/flipAnim/drawJavelin/drawArrow/polyPath — all consumed); today's surfaces fully wired (fpSim → drawHitboxes only, by design — debug draws sim truth; aSp/bSp/fSp → stills + harness recomputes; info().flip/flipT → flipproof); all three shot-kind pre-wires live (the javelin/page/scroll rows emit them); no hot-path regression (leadFlip ≈ 4 property reads per substep call — the ×1-exact fast paths keep unflagged geometry bit-stable).

SPEC/CONTRACT/BUILD verified aligned — SPEC's weapon-art row still correctly reads NOT LOCKED and defers to GAMES.md (the convention close is the next session; nothing else stale there). **Trim (retired VERBATIM to CHRONICLE-archive's "Retired at the round-4 trim" section, tombstone left in place):** the two superseded re-presentation-glint paragraphs of the edge-leading entry (the mechanism was removed the same day it shipped — the tombstone names its two permanent outcomes: the build-stamp convention and the FX contrast-check lesson) and the "Canonical file cleaned; the nunchaku commission is paste-ready" current-state bullet (superseded: batch-4 delivered against current canonical, and the snapshot it referenced was purged at round 3). **Workspace purge:** the four spent rider briefs at root (`javelin/tome/scroll/orb-artist-brief.md` — design sheets and observed-records live in their entries; future commissions rebuild fresh riders against canonical, the house pattern) · the four accepted delivery originals (`deliveries/spinclash-preview {javelin,tome,scroll,orb}.html` — each promotion diff-proven, canonical is the source record per the flail precedent; `deliveries/` keeps its intake README) · the ten spent ritual fingerprints (`fp-pre/post-{javelin, headlead, tome, scroll, orb}` — round-2 precedent: **`.qa/fp-after.json` remains the sole baseline**; every delta proof is recorded inline in its entry) · `scratch/` entire (the four gauntlet birth records — javelin/tome/scroll/orb — their numbers live in the entries and the pattern lives in leadproof/fingerprint; plus the review's insurance snapshot, after verification).
This entry's named purge supersedes the four "kept as the birth record" sentences in today's gauntlet entries (the round-2 amendment-by-name pattern). **Retained:** all 11 active `.qa` tools + the sole baseline (inscribe, fingerprint, killstill, hitstill, reveal-probe, projectile-fix, natscan, boomdiag, bake-circles-v2 [CIRCLES_V2 provenance], leadproof, flipproof, fp-after.json), the three root harnesses, the six docs, the `deliveries/` intake. Post-cleanup verification: fingerprints 8/8 byte-identical · inscription gate PASS all 22 · flipproof + leadproof ALL PASS · chromecheck PASS (one run tripped the tool's documented natural-seed timing flake — disambiguated by the recipe's re-run rule, 4/4 consecutive PASS). **The roster is complete at 22 and the record is the single source of truth — next: the weapon-art convention close (five ratifications), then the balance & skill rework opening with the sanctioned measure run.**

**Base freeze + reconcile pre-staging (2026-10-23 — the full-roster redesign/sigil commission is IN PROGRESS in the owner's Claude session, pasted against `build thrash-r11`, md5 `e9662ebf9d038b5d974a5282fad59e8b`).** While the artist works, nothing touches the canonical, `roster-redesign-brief.md`, or behaviour-describing doc lines: any canonical edit would desync the artist's base, trip the brief's five STALE-BASE markers, and force the truncation-resplice fallback. **Sigil mechanism pre-wire + the roster-redesign brief (2026-10-23, owner decisions: "a motif is good, but i dont want it to be like all weapons have the same dot or similar, i want it to make sense for the weapons … dont just invent something on the sword just for the sake of givinig it a color, like put a string or something stupid on the sword, which doesnt make sense" and "i think claude should redesign the weapons, as it has done a good job so far, and it is also faster that way, waht do you think ? we should create a prompt to give it").** The carrier-mark mechanism, renderer-only and inert until used: `const KC = '@carrier'` beside the palette consts; `drawWeapon` AND `drawWeaponChain` (both fill passes — the chain branch was the one to not forget, the flail/nunchaku bands are sigil candidates) swap `pl.fill === KC` → the carrier's `COLS[f.idx]`, gold fallback for carrier-less renders (the contact-sheet tool, any direct call); `acc` threaded through `drawWeaponOn`/`drawWeaponOf`/`drawHitboxes`; `fillOverride` still wins (the poster's tinted silhouettes and the debug red stay uniform).
Fail-visibly by design: `KC` is an invalid CSS color, so an unswapped sigil renders broken rather than silently gold. Proofs: fingerprints **8/8 byte-identical** (no art emits `KC` yet), chromecheck PASS, gate PASS 22, leadproof ALL PASS. Stamp **build marks-r10** — the brief's primary STALE-BASE marker. **`roster-redesign-brief.md` at the repo root:** the full-roster commission — (A) sigils for all 22 via a 22-line **mark map** under the recolor-first doctrine (~16 weapons recolor an existing plausible element: the shield's gold star device, staff/wand gems, shuriken hub, katana tsuba, scythe ferrule, nunchaku/flail bands, knives' binding bar, javelin's grip binding, tome's strap, orb's rune band; ~6 add the smallest plausible interior element: hammer head inlay, dagger maker's mark — explicitly NOT a band near the body, the owner's step1h veto stands — axe socket band, sword maker's mark, spear lashing, mace boss inlay, boomerang wing band, and a scroll wax seal with the ribbon staying the owner's KR red), and (B) owner-directed in-session redesigns of whichever weapons he names from his contact-sheet review (`scratch/sheet.mjs` → `scratch/roster-sheet-v1.svg`: all 22 at true played scale, spine horizontal, body-disc ghost for tuck/scale context, trait/shot labels — rendered through the game's own drawWeapon via recording ctx).
The brief carries the ghosting rule (silhouette change ⇒ DELETE that `CIRCLES_V2` row and list the deletions; interior-only sigil ⇒ row stays valid and must NOT be deleted), the hard invariants (zero sim/renderer lines; uniform bases; len ≤ LT and only at owner word; traits travel with the art — a moved business contour flips its `lead`/`headLead` sign with an `observed:` note; chain `CH` segs/j coherence is in-scope art-coupled data; launcher `off` re-tune on the tome/scroll precedent; tangential grips at t = 0.5; WEAPON_IDS frozen; shot ART out of scope), and the reconcile contract: rebake every ghosted weapon, gate all 22, **leadproof PROBES re-quoted if katana/scythe/javelin art moved** (they cite art coordinates), the fingerprint ritual with per-weapon surgical deltas, forced-cell gauntlets for changed weapons, the suite, and the **shot-accent companion** — the projectile half of the owner's decision (form b: one accent element per shot kind in the carrier's color; the per-kind element map gets proposed at reconcile, the scroll's flying ribbon keeping its KR unless he says otherwise).
Session pending at this writing; the delivery's reconcile entry records the outcome. The palette-redo lane stays separate and later (owner: "redo all the colors of the bots … easy recongnizable stable clors like red, green, blue, purple" — the sigil mechanism reads `COLS`, so every mark updates automatically when that session lands).

**Thrash-volley fix — the deep-disengage gate (2026-10-23, owner option 1 from the presented menu: "do 1", N=90; sim-data change, fingerprints re-baselined, stamp build thrash-r11).** Owner playtest complaint (seed 24267, screenshot): "speedys weapon is continuously touching heavys body, so heavy does not take damage, while heavys weapon is also getting parried consecutively, resulting in the weapon going back and forth damaging speedy multiple times as the weapon leaves and reenters speedys body, this looks very ugly … essentially nothing wrong with it mechanically except it looks ugly". Diagnosis (`scratch/thrashdiag.mjs`): round 1 was entirely LEGAL — t313 Speedy's hammer hit + latch across Heavy (no further damage, law 4 correct), t385–452 a four-clang parry volley (all >16-tick flutter gate), then t473/517/568 Heavy's javelin left and re-entered Speedy's body twice more inside 95 ticks — every re-entry a fresh legal hit under the old 12px-clearance re-arm — an HP-number and flash stutter. Population (40 naturals, 684 consecutive same-attacker blade-hit pairs): 662 genuine re-hits cleared ≥80px (interval median 394 ticks); the thrash class was 4 pairs (0.6%) at ≤21px clearance, 13–28-tick intervals, parries inside — bimodal in clearance, middle bands sparse. Menu presented: (1) shallow-reentry cooldown — re-arm needs pad AND (clearance ≥48px OR ≥N ticks), N=90 recommended / 120 calmer; (2) reArmPad 12→48 (also silences slow shallow genuine re-hits); (3) pure pair timer (rejected — eats genuine fast exchanges); (4) accept as spectacle. Owner: "do 1" (N=90).

Built: `CFG.reArmDeep` + `CFG.reArmWait` 90; per-fighter `bladeLatchTick`/`bladeClearPeak` (written at every latch, reset at every round roll and fighter init, never read unstuck); the gate in `bladeVsBody`'s stuck branch — peak updated BEFORE the check (a single-substep exit blowing straight through the band re-arms on the spot), re-arm = clear > reArmPad AND (peak ≥ reArmDeep OR G.t − latchTick ≥ reArmWait). A buried blade can never re-arm (the pad clause fails), so one-touch-one-damage stays structural; the gate rides the LATCH, so knock-only contacts (projectile spines, the shield) are gated identically; solidity untouched — the resolver runs every substep regardless, this gates EVENTS only, the same division of labour as law 2's parryGate. `measure.mjs`'s law-4 replica mirrors the block exactly (replica state arrays + roll reset + the same substep order; header and report lines quote the constants generically so recalibration propagates without further edits) — edited, NOT run (owner-go rule; the parity check is `stuckFlagMismatch` at the next sanctioned run). Stills unaffected (they latch off events; gated hits simply never emit).

**The in-session calibration correction — the diagnosis was incomplete.** The first pass shipped `reArmDeep` 48, placed in what the population scan called the empty middle band between the ≤21px thrash class and the ≥80px genuine band. At 48 the class died in the population (thrash-shaped pairs 0/693) BUT seed 24267 round 1 replayed byte-identical — the owner's episode survived. `scratch/gatecheck.mjs` (substep truth: every latch episode reconstructed off `auditSubs` — peak clearance, branch taken, re-arm tick) showed why: at 48 every episode re-armed out the DEEP branch ~5 ticks after its hit, the recorded peak censored at the threshold (48.1–48.7px — the branch cuts the reconstruction off). The 85-override exposed the true amplitudes: **the episode rocks 69–79px peaks** — it was the middle band all along; the ≤21px/13–28t class was the NATURALS' thrash, a different animal with the same shape. The threshold moved above the whole middle band: `reArmDeep` 48 → 85, mechanism and N=90 exactly as approved (the flipMs/scythe-sign tuning precedent — constants tuned until the approved behaviour is actually delivered; owner veto window open). Cost proof (`scratch/gatecheck-partB.txt`, pair census at both thresholds): the [80,85) band is EMPTY at both; middle-band fast pairs (48–80px, interval <90t) went **8 → 0**; every surviving <90t pair clears ≥91px — the genuine fast exchanges option 3 would have eaten, kept by design; knock physics already forces 696/701 natural re-hits past 120px, so the wait branch binds only in cornered/rocking fights. Episode at the final build: hits t473 → t568 → t682 (95/114-tick intervals, wait branch, peaks 79.2/69.4px) — distinct pokes with parries between, exactly the approved cadence.

Final population (`scratch/thrash-post-r11.txt`): 701 pairs over seeds 1–40; thrash-shaped (≤32px, ≤90t, parry inside) **0/701**; the middle band holds one survivor at a 97-tick interval (a wait-branch poke, legal by design); the genuine band 700 pairs ≥80px, interval median 399 ticks (was 394 pre-fix). Ritual: `.qa/fp-pre-thrash.json` (the pre-gate world, retained) → `.qa/fp-post-thrash.json` (final deep-85 build): **0/8 byte-identical** — every melee timeline shifts its re-arm moments (`bladeStuck` hashes per tick), the chaos-ceiling delta shape. Tick counts: boomerang+shuriken s1 12273→12251 (−0.2%), hammer+dagger s5 12601→13501 (+7.1%), shield+hammer s4 12070→11669 (−3.3%), staff+hammer s1 9325→7380 (−20.9%, the largest drift — a launcher cell whose spine-knock latches are now gated, changing the knock cadence), natural s7 same length 13664 with a drifted hash, the three known capped cells hold 18000 (bow+crossbow s2, wand+bow s3, natural s11). `.qa/fp-after.json` re-baselined := post; the intermediate deep-48 baseline was superseded in-session (its own 0/8 diff had the same shape; staff+hammer sat at 8788 there). Suite (final build): chromecheck PASS · inscription gate PASS 22 · killstill ALL PASS (24 kills / 14 seeds — blade 18, stone 6; gContact 24/24) · hitstill ALL PASS (gContact 469/469; the invisible-at-drawn-poses class 3/259; stone landings 210/210) · reveal-probe ALL PASS · projectile-fix PROBE PASS (A: **0** melee hits by projectile weapons across 1312 spine-shove latch edges; B 0/6596; C 0/245,492; D informational 4/18) · leadproof ALL PASS · flipproof ALL PASS (31/31 episodes land exactly ±1). Recorded, not judged (rework-window material): cornered/pinned fights now poke at the ≥90-tick (0.75s) cadence instead of buzzing — short weapons' grind DPS is relatively lower than pre-gate, and the time fallback makes any clearance-unreachable re-hit cadence-based. **Cornered-poke residual — judged NOT critical (owner review 2026-10-23: "dont code answer if its critial or not", then "yet just note it down in the docs, but only short like one line"): rare, self-limiting, one-touch-one-damage intact — left as-is, no code.** **Doc accident, repaired same day:** the Current-state bullet insertion truncated the neighbouring parry-flutter-gate bullet (a fuzzy-match edit consumed its first 200 chars and spliced its tail onto the new bullet's line); no text was lost — the region was rejoined surgically and verified byte-identical (the split fell inside "e|xtension"); the lesson re-records the house gotcha: never anchor an edit on `cut -c`-truncated output. The redesign brief's STALE-BASE markers moved with the stamp (marker 1 now `build thrash-r11`, new marker 5: `CFG` contains `reArmDeep`). **Playtest pending — the acceptance test is the owner's eye on seed 24267: the volley should read as distinct pokes, and genuine exchanges should feel unchanged everywhere else.**

**Hygiene pass — doc navigation, the 2026-10-19 retirement, and the harness-speed retarget (2026-10-23, owner pick "hygiene" from the base-freeze menu; zero code changes: the canonical was never touched — md5 `e9662ebf9d038b5d974a5282fad59e8b`, stamp `thrash-r11`, brief md5 `d51444b17303e1639da7c0c33c89de84` — and `measure.mjs` was edited, NOT run).**

- **Navigation layer.** GAMES.md had grown to 246 KB whose Measured-facts entries were single lines of up to **13 KB** — unreadable in any editor. Two new `scratch/` tools fix that permanently: `navgen.mjs` regenerates the `## How to read this file` block in place (parts table + the entry index, iterated to a fixed point so its quoted line numbers are true of the file it writes; `--check` verifies without writing) and `docreflow.mjs` splits the monster paragraphs — 14 of them, >3.5 KB each, into 74 — as a **whitespace-only edit that refuses to write unless four proofs hold**: the whole-file non-whitespace byte stream, the word stream, every line's `**`/backtick parity, and the entry-heading count. Verified after the pass: non-whitespace content **identical outside the nav block**; both tools idempotent under `--check`. Two defects of the first attempt are recorded because they became guards: breaks landed **inside bold spans**, and two adjacent same-day entries were **merged** (entries are not blank-separated in this file) — the entry detector now requires a *date* in the line-leading bold span, which is also what fixed the index count.
- **Retirement (owner pick: the 2026-10-19 era only).** Eleven Measured-facts entries moved VERBATIM to `CHRONICLE-archive.md` → "Retired at the 2026-10-23 hygiene pass"; a tombstone carrying their still-load-bearing numbers (the first sanctioned v1.x run, the solidity tier's 7m18s birth run and law-3's 5.568px worst, `revealTicks` 285, hitstop 60/400, `parryPush` 150 → 525 → 700, the K.O. still, the inscribe fix's doctrine, the code-review pass's two fixes) opens the section. **GAMES.md 246 → 233 KB.** The 169-cell-matrix DEFERRED entry was deliberately KEPT — it is a live owner decision, not a record. Cross-reference audit first: three live pointers found and updated (the Current-state K.O./inscribe bullet, the REACH fix's back-reference, The-documents' description of the archive).
- **Harness speed — retargeted, not cut (owner pick 1).** Finding: there is no code win available. `measure.mjs` already loads the sim once per worker, steals battles through an atomic cursor, and defaults `jobs = availableParallelism()`; the sandbox's ≈11 min is core count (2), and the wall clock is dominated by the few battles that exhaust `BUDGET_SEC` 420 — a capped battle costs ≈7× a typical one and the cap class is ~10 of the 23 cells plus the cap-prone naturals. So the ~10-minute line is now documented as the **owner's-machine target, which is met today** (≈3–4 min at 6–8 cores). No coverage was reduced; the three rejected levers and their risks are recorded in the Testing recipe's tier guide so the choice is not re-litigated blind. Edits: comment-only in `measure.mjs` (header + the `SOLIDITY_CELLS` orb note), which stays edited-NOT-run.

Audit pass #1 caught the trap twice (behaviour-diff section + sim-denylist section) → the STOP+disclose path is rehearsed; after trap-revert + CR-normalization, pass #2 was clean: exit 0, sigil table 22 ids (katana ×1), ghost list `wand`. The normalization step proved content-only — re-inserting CRs reproduced the redelivery byte-for-byte. Gates on the copy: pre-bake inscribe flagged wand `n=0 MISS-FAIL` (the expected ghost, logged not panicked); post-bake PASS (wand n=18 poke −0.30 miss 2.68; katana's KEPT row n=25 poke −0.30 miss 2.77 — the interior-only path verified). leadproof + flipproof ALL PASS (~18s each), the rollsFlagged tripwire silent. Step 8a mechanics rehearsed: the `COLS` → `[KG, KB]` one-liner is scope-safe (the single main `<script>` spans lines 91–2634; palette consts at line 163, `COLS` at 1722, same scope).
- **Toolcraft facts, lost with the tools (re-derive if rebuilt):** the four audit defects found and fixed during rehearsal were CR noise polluting diff hunks; comment lines tripping the sim denylist (observed: a note containing "CFG.flipMs 180 present…"); the flail `j` regex returning null = a false pass; and the sigil table matching only 4 of 22 rows because canonical `WEAPONS` rows are non-uniform (alignment spaces `sword:  {`, multi-line objects, chain data external in `FLAIL_CH`/`NUNCHAKU_CH`). argv surfaces: fingerprint `argv[3]`; inscribe takes args after `--regen`; leadproof/flipproof/killstill/hitstill/projectile-fix/reveal-probe `argv[2]`; chromecheck `argv[2]` (relative to its dir); sheet-v2 `argv[2]` file / `argv[3]` out / `argv[4]`,`argv[5]` accents. `inscribe --regen` iterates `WEAPON_IDS` (a trimmed-WEAPON_IDS temp prints only the ghost rows); regen output carries no chain `s` ties (ties are boot-time geometric, tie v2) and chains need per-segment multi-regen merges (the flail pattern) — a row-splice baker does NOT cover chains. The pipe-exit-code trap re-confirmed: `node x | tee; echo $?` reports tee's status — run audits without a pipe (or via PIPESTATUS).
- **Not done at the cut (never landed anywhere):** the two fingerprint diffs (`fp-delivery` vs `.qa/fp-after.json` — expect only wand-attributable sim moves; `fp-8a` vs `fp-delivery` — must be 8/8 byte-identical, the renderer-only proof), the step-11 sheet-v2 gate-interpretation rehearsal (expect mixed katana ×1 / rest ×0), the truncation-splice fallback rehearsal, the dry-run `report.md`, the runbook amendments, and this file's entry. ### `scratch/paste-ledger.md` (verbatim, as purged)

# paste ledger — who owns each roster row (living, re-measured on every tool run)

> **STATUS 2026-10-28 — HISTORY, not a live instruction.** The landing this
> ledger tracked **executed 2026-10-25** (see the PROMOTION RECORD at the end:
> "Owed still: none"), and the roster landed at roster-r12. Three corrections so
> nothing below is taken as current:
> 1. `scratch/artist-kit.html` is **QUARANTINED** (hygiene pass #1): its
>    thrash-r11 base predates orb-r14 + sweep-r15, so it still teaches retired
>    gold `KG` and a pre-32-gon orb. Do not paste it. Rebuild owed at the next
>    commission kickoff, re-seeded from the landed canonical art.
> 2. The two landing tools this loop names — `scratch/kit-intake-audit.mjs` and
>    `scratch/kit-splice-staging.mjs` — were **PURGED** at hygiene pass #1
>    (2026-10-28) as spent one-shots, per the close's own doctrine ("runbooks
>    retire per delivery and rebuild fresh") and the `bake-circles-v2`
>    precedent. The gates and the draw-units ÷1.3 conversion they encoded are
>    described below and in `claude-design-prompt.md`, which is what a rebuild
>    starts from. The standing row-splice baker is `.qa/bake-rows.mjs`.

## gates a pasted block is measured against (no silent fixes)

1. `KC` count === 1 — exactly one diegetic sigil, never on `KP`.
2. radial weapons: art `maxD` ≤ LT = 113.44 (pre-scale units).
3. mace: `maxD` must equal LT — it is the sentinel every `len: LT` row inherits.
4. keepers (`cls K`): drawn-union mask must stay pixel-identical, else the row ghosts and the
   ledger + landing list move (that is information, not a failure — the owner may want it).
5. scale is **detected**: the audit divides `maxD` by `S` (and by `KX` for the crossbow), so a
   pre-scale INGR block lands as-is; if the extent matches the canonical `len` under neither
   reading the paste is ambiguous → ask, never guess.
6. launch anchors stay where they shipped (bow off 26, crossbow off 34, knives off 38, tome
   off 10, scroll off 11, staff/wand `fire: 'tip'`, chain joint distances).
7. canonical `spinclash-preview.html` md5 before === after (read-only proof).

10 invalidated rows → delete + row-splice rebake. `reconcile-runbook.md` step 5 currently

### `scratch/claude-design-prompt.md` (verbatim, as purged)

# SPINCLASH — weapon artist commission v2 (kit route, one weapon per message)

> v1 (2026-10-25 era) pasted the 244 KB canonical `spinclash-preview.html` into
> the session. Superseded 2026-10-24 at owner vote: the base is now the small
> self-contained **artist kit** (`scratch/artist-kit.html`, ~39 KB) — same
> medium, same conventions, paste-able. The kit is generated from the live
> proposal set; its blocks are canonical `build([...])` grammar in draw units.

You are the weapon artist for SPINCLASH, a dependency-free browser physics
auto-battler. Fighters are discs (bodyR 64) that spin a weapon around
themselves; the art is read at a glance, in motion, at roughly 60–150 px.

The owner pastes into this conversation: **this prompt + the complete artist
kit**. The kit is the single source of truth for the medium: conventions
header, helpers (`rect` / `star` / `build`), the 22 `INGR_*` blocks, the
`IDENTITY` table, and a self-viewer. Read it before designing. If anything
below disagrees with the kit, the kit wins — say so.

## The loop

1. On receiving this prompt, reply in **3 lines or fewer**: acknowledge the
   rules and ask which weapon. Do not design anything yet. The roster (22):
   `bow, hammer, dagger, axe, sword, spear, mace, shield, staff, wand,
   crossbow, boomerang, shuriken, flail, nunchaku, scythe, katana, knives,
   javelin, tome, scroll, orb`.
2. The owner names **one** weapon. You design that weapon only — never others,
   never the roster in bulk. He iterates in-session ("fatter head", "sigil
   elsewhere"); the owner IS the question loop.
3. You reply with the 3-part delivery: **note** (what the look does, one
   paragraph) → **code** (the complete `const INGR_<ID> = build([...])` block,
   draw units, drop-in replacement for that weapon's kit block) → **audit
   line** (measured, not claimed): `KC ×1 · reach <n> vs LT 147.47 · anchors
   kept/moved-with-note · traits kept/flipped-with-note · chain coherent/n-a`.
4. In-session revisions may emit only the changed block + fresh audit. The
   **final delivery is the COMPLETE kit file** with every accepted change in
   place — the owner hands that file to the implementer whole.

## Rules (the kit header carries the full doctrine; these are the hard edges)

- **Edit only the 22 `INGR_*` blocks and their `observed:` header lines.**
  Conventions, helpers, `IDENTITY`, viewer, palette consts: frozen context.
  No reformatting of untouched blocks — the implementer diff-audits your
  return against the pristine base, and formatting noise hides real deltas.
- **Geometry is yours.** Silhouettes may change freely (that is the point of
  the pass). Hitboxes are NOT yours: never author covering circles; a changed
  silhouette ghosts until the implementer rebakes it — designed cost, not a
  defect. `IDENTITY` (len / hold / fire anchor / lead / headLead) is shipped
  truth: row values change only at the owner's in-session word, and a moved
  business contour flips its `lead`/`headLead` sign WITH the art plus an
  `observed:` note (katana edge −1, scythe beak −1, javelin head +1).
- **Sigil doctrine:** exactly ONE diegetic element per weapon wears `KC` —
  never on a `KP` face, never a sticker, recolor-first. The seeded block's
  header names the current sigil; keep the doctrine when art moves around it.
- **Caps:** d-axis extent (reach) ≤ LT = 147.47 draw units, every weapon; the
  mace envelope is the sentinel — its reach EQUALS LT, never move it.
  Launchers keep their declared exit (bow off 33.8, crossbow 44.2, knives 49.4,
  tome 13, scroll 14.3, staff/wand `fire:'tip'`); tangential grips stay at the
  chord middle. Chain weapons: segment shape-ranges + joint d's stay coherent
  with any re-authored shapes; counts and hinge order never change.
- **Medium:** flat fills, locked palette, no gradients/texture/glow/opacity;
  the renderer adds the only ink (3px round-join around every shape); 4–14
  shapes; nothing solid thinner than ~1.5 draw units (thinner = paint, third
  element `1`); curves as generously-sampled polylines; hand-made, not
  generated (tapers, asymmetry, material logic).
- **The `chain:` header line is MACHINE-READ** (flail, nunchaku): the owner's
  arena page and the implementer's splice parse segs + joints from it, so keep
  the kit's exact format — `chain: segs shapes a-b / c / …, joints at d x / y
  / z` — and update it in the same breath as any re-counted shape list. The
  arena then re-maps segments live and swings your new art; a *moved joint*
  is sim data: the arena refuses it (kept staged) and the implementer
  reconciles it at landing.
- **Never generate, request or reference images.** You design blind from
  coordinates and the doctrine; the owner's eyes live on the kit's self-viewer
  and the implementer's in-game arena page.
- **No questions in the final delivery.** Judgment calls that survive get
  one-line `observed: …` notes in that weapon's header.
- Determinism: no `Math.random` / `Date.now` / `performance.now` anywhere.
  `WEAPON_IDS` frozen: same 22, same order.

## Why the file you return is safe to hand over

The implementer splices your blocks verbatim into the game's staging set,
runs the seven gates (KC ×1 · radial reach ≤ LT · mace sentinel · keeper-union
pixel identity · scale detection · launch anchors · canonical md5 unchanged),
re-renders the review sheet and the in-game arena page, re-bakes every ghosted
covering, and lands the roster in one batch after the owner's playtest. Your
blocks are canonical grammar, so nothing is re-authored downstream — what you
emit is what ships.

## Shots — the flying art (owner rulings 2026-10-24, scope: all kinds)

The kit's SHOTS zone lists every kind with its launcher, hitbox `r` and speed,
plus today's look. Rules beyond the weapon doctrine:

- **Shot-local grammar:** origin = projectile centre, +x = flight direction,
  DRAW units, `build([...])`. Spinning kinds (spark, spell) spin in code.
- **Accent doctrine (owner vote):** exactly ONE diegetic accent element per
  kind wears `KC` — dyed fletching, cord wrap, glyph core. Never the whole
  body, never on a KP edge. The shot must still read as *what* it is.
- **Stay inside `r`** (the halo disc inks the true hitbox; visible-contact-
  first applies in flight). Art that must exceed it: disclose the whiff band
  in the header, measured.
- **Copy kinds are NOT yours:** star, boom, javelin fly as the wielder's own
  weapon polys by engine rule — redesigning the weapon redesigns its throw.
  Never author SHOT_STAR / SHOT_BOOM / SHOT_JAVELIN.
- **knife is a new kind** (splits off bolt): ONE knife, the exact look of one
  of the weapon's three knives. (The owner is considering a 3-knife volley —
  one per fan direction — as a mechanics ruling; design the single knife so a
  volley of three reads clean.)
- **spell is shared** by tome AND scroll: one flat rune-glyph visual, no glow,
  no gradient. The old page sheet and parchment roll are retired by it.
- Deliver shot blocks as `const SHOT_<KIND> = build([...])` inside the SHOTS
  zone, header line per block: `/* shot <kind> · r <n> · accent: <element> ·
  observed: … */`. Audit line adds: `r-fit ok/disclosed`.
- **Paint flag for shots (lever 2):** third element `1` = no-ink paint. Glow
  layers = simplified silhouette offsets of the head region behind the body
  (array-first), mid KB / outer KP, fixed tints, never carrier-mixed, never
  gradient/opacity. Weapon blocks must not use the flag. Muzzle flash and
  speed-stretch are implementer-side — never author them.
- **Animation contract (owner 2026-10-25, levers 1+2):** third-element flags on
  shots: `1` = glow paint (no ink) · `2` = alternate frame of the preceding
  shape, no ink · `3` = alternate frame, inked. Alt pairs swap every 4 ticks
  (two-frame flicker). Flicker scope: glow layers and the OUTER tongue only —
  inner structure stays stable so the silhouette never mushes. Implementer
  dials (glow pulse, tail sway, speed-stretch) apply themselves: never author
  them. DOCTRINE: animate the tail and the glow, NEVER the head — the head
  carries the hitbox disc and the eye anchors contact truth on it.

was renamed `cycleIdx` because the state is no longer tome-specific; and `fireShots` lost its last four `f.weapon === …` tests.
Behaviour-identical, proven: fingerprints 8/8 byte-identical vs `.qa/fp-lead-r22.json`, natscan 30/30 seeds' sim fields identical
(total ticks 891,003 both sides), cellscan and projectile-fix identical, a 22/22-row launch-equivalence probe, and a 3464-record
shot-kind/launch-point probe over 8 cells covering all ten launchers. Canonical `8ad8e17325b24849b1f86efce5ebace5` →
`54ab0e5b784962987fdec6cc71cd6636`, no stamp bump (build stays `lead-r22`). Full record: GAMES.md, the pre-skills cleanup entry.
