## Retired doc text — README's Status and commission-pipeline paragraphs (2026-10-28, code-review + doc-alignment pass; verbatim, append-only)

Retired because they had gone stale in place and contradicted README's own rule that it "deliberately repeats none of" GAMES.md: the Status still named `roster-r12` as the landed build (the shipped build is `sigil-r23`), named only the 2026-10-22 cleanup, still described the **balance pass as deferred** when its rulings landed 2026-10-28 (the skills pass is what is next), and did not reflect the sigil-legibility treatment. The commission paragraph was roughly 60% inline amendment about tools already purged. Replaced by a current-facts Status; the history it summarised lives in GAMES.md's dated entries. Verbatim:

### Paragraph 1 — "Blueprint complete…"

Blueprint complete; **no engine code yet** — start at Phase 0 in `BUILD.md`. Game #1 (SPINCLASH)'s frame (the PEBBLE look) is locked; its **weapon art LANDED at build roster-r12** (2026-10-25 landing of the full-roster redesign + sigil + shot commissions; the weapon-art convention CLOSED 2026-10-27 (GAMES.md, "Weapon art — the closed convention")).

### Paragraph 2 — "The running code is…"

The running code is `spinclash-preview.html` — Game #1's design preview: a dependency-free browser sim (pure `newGame`/`tick` + renderer + chrome, harness-extractable) with a **22-weapon roster — COMPLETE** (the v0.9–v1.2 rigid batch, the **flail** (2026-10-21), **batch-4: nunchaku, scythe, katana, knives (2026-10-22)**, the **javelin** (plus its owner-requested `headLead` head-flip), the **tome** (2026-10-22), the **scroll** (2026-10-23, first per-weapon reference colours) and the **magic orb** (2026-10-23, the shaft-less true sentinel)) — the Core contact law + the seven shot laws, render interpolation, the overflow-band canvas (weapon ink never cuts), hitstop as a true pause of the on-screen frame (kill still on the K.O. hold) under the standing visible-contact-first directive, the owner-designed VS-poster dice reveal, and the 2026-10-21 feel-tuned physics set (gravity 1400 · chaos ceiling 1200 with 0.9 wall bleed · parryPush 700 · the parry flutter gate · the capped, tick-gated pin-escape nudge). The chain sim is **N-segment generic** (`ch.np`, ≤3 particles) and now carries two chain weapons (flail 3 particles, nunchaku 1). A whole-file code-review cleanup (2026-10-22) left the canonical file behaviour-identical, fingerprints byte-identical; the batch-4 delivery was reconciled the same day (per-segment bake, inscription gate PASS on all 18, gauntlet + fast tier green, fingerprints re-baselined — GAMES.md, last Measured-facts entries); and the **edge-leading mirror** shipped for directionally asymmetric art (katana and scythe — the business contour, cutting edge or hook beak, always leads the motion; art and hitbox mirrored together on spin sign; owner observation, 2026-10-22; **the dagger and the staff joined them 2026-10-28** at owner playtest — "the dagger is not symmetrical, it has this white line which hints which way it is facing" — so four rows now carry `lead: -1`, found by a 22-row facing audit in GAMES.md, build `lead-r22`) with the sibling **`headLead`** trait for along-chord asymmetry (the javelin's head end always points the way the grip travels), its parry re-presentation animated as a fast **flip transition** — the blade eases through edge-on like a spin about its own spine, `CFG.flipMs` 180ms (owner preference, replacing the instant snap and the removed glint; art-only — the sim, stills and hitbox overlay keep the binary truth; generic for every future `lead` weapon, renderer-only). The **sigil** doctrine (one diegetic carrier-colored element per weapon — owner doctrine 2026-10-23) is REALIZED across all 22 at roster-r12, alongside the shot program: nine flying kinds (1:1 munitions, the copy rule for star/boom/javelin, the staff orb and wand spark under the whole-body waiver, the scroll's fire comet and the tome's poison/frost/fire trio whose affinity colours pre-figure the skill pass), the knives' three-knife volley (cadence ×3, measured tip anchors, spine-frame axes), and the launch-doctrine corrections the landing exposed. The commission ran through the artist-kit route (*amended 2026-10-28, hygiene pass #2: `scratch/artist-kit.html` and `scratch/claude-design-prompt.md` were purged at the pre-balance checkpoint — the brief and the paste ledger are retired VERBATIM to `CHRONICLE-archive.md`, "Retired at the 2026-10-28 pre-balance checkpoint", and the kit itself is recorded there by digest; the `deliveries/` returns folder is not part of this checkpoint*); the convention's prescriptive lock now lives in GAMES.md, "Weapon art — the closed convention" (ratified 2026-10-27; the close draft retired at the ratification).

### Paragraph 3 — "New weapons arrive…"

New weapons arrive through the **commission pipeline**: a small self-contained **artist kit** (generated against staging, conventions + helpers + identity table + self-viewer inside; **PURGED 2026-10-28 at the pre-balance checkpoint — it had been QUARANTINED that same day: its thrash-r11 base predated orb-r14/sweep-r15, so it still taught retired gold `KG` and a pre-32-gon orb**) pasted into an artist session with the design brief (`scratch/claude-design-prompt.md`, purged with it — both retired VERBATIM to `CHRONICLE-archive.md`, "Retired at the 2026-10-28 pre-balance checkpoint", alongside the paste ledger, `redesign-preview.mjs`, `sheet-v2.mjs` and `design-shots.json`; **the kit-route rebuild stays owed at the next commission kickoff and now re-seeds from that archive plus the landed canonical art, and must carry the placement/reach rules published 2026-10-28 in GAMES.md's closed convention ("Placement & reach": both rim mounts, the 61.44 px tuck and its hidden-ink budget, the two closed-form reach formulas, the derived no-tuck rule for tangential chords, and the boot equalization factors — plus `WDEF`, the weapon-row defaults, so a new row declares only what is special about it)**); returns land in `deliveries/`, intake-audit against the pristine base (frozen zones, KC ×1, caps, chain-line coherence), splice into the staging set, arena + sheet re-render (*the staging tool, `sheet-v2` and `redesign-preview` were purged 2026-10-28 — this step is rebuilt by the kit route; the arena bench page itself was kept*), then one batch landing: row-splice rebake (`.qa/bake-rows.mjs`) → inscription gate → leadproof/flipproof → fingerprint ritual → gauntlet + suite → owner playtest. The rider-brief era (`roster-redesign-brief.md`: batch-4 through orb) is retired verbatim to `CHRONICLE-archive.md`. The first four-weapon batch (nunchaku rider + scythe/katana/knives) was delivered and reconciled 2026-10-22, the **javelin** and **tome** (first two single riders; the tome consumed its pre-wired `page` shot kind with zero renderer edits) followed the same day, and the **scroll** (the owner's ancient-scroll direction: first per-weapon reference colours, burst:3 mechanic deferred whole to the rework) and the **magic orb** (2026-10-23 — the true-sentinel look as a shaft-less radial weapon: a floating orb, zero new mechanics, owner-iterated orbit at len 54) after that — **ROSTER COMPLETE AT 22 AND LANDED at roster-r12, the commission queue is empty**; the convention **CLOSED 2026-10-27** (owner vote on the full sheet), so next is **the balance & skill rework** (GAMES.md open question 1). The **balance pass** stays deferred until the mechanics set is complete (shield purpose, pure-projectile-mirror length — GAMES.md open questions).

## Retired Measured-facts entries — the pre-2026-10-28 ledger (2026-10-28, code-review + doc-alignment pass; owner pick "retire the pre-2026-10-28 entries"; verbatim, append-only)

Thirteen entries retired from `uploads/GAMES.md`'s Measured facts, each leaving a one-line indexed pointer there. Retired because their subject had closed (the paste-loop commission route, the art-convention ratification, the 2026-10-27 balance-menu landings) and their live residue is carried in GAMES.md's live sections and in the canonical's own comments. Nothing was summarised or edited: the text below is byte-verbatim, and every number a later entry quotes from them still resolves here. Where an entry recorded a mechanism that is still live, its pointer in GAMES.md says so and names the surviving constant.

**The 2026-10-19 era — eleven entries retired VERBATIM to `CHRONICLE-archive.md` (2026-10-23 hygiene pass, owner pick; the section now opens at the 2026-10-21 REACH fix).** What that day settled, and where the full numbers live:

- **The first sanctioned `measure.mjs` run of the v1.x era** (`--quick`, 2 jobs): 42 battles · determinism PASS · **all 13 law checks PASS** · termination 39/40 · winner spread speedy 15 / heavy 24 · battle median 123.0s · events 5.6/s · **stall worst 7.50s — FAIL vs the 5s line** (v0.8.3's red was 5.29s) · buzzsaw 0/40 · max dmg 19 · shot ledger 7919 thrown / 828 landed (10.5%). Entries: *v1.2 baseline, part 0* + *Fast-probe re-run post-CIRCLES_V2*.
- **The solidity tier's birth run**: 40 naturals + the 12-cell overlap gauntlet × 3 seeds = 76 battles, **7m18s** at 2 sandbox jobs, all 13 laws PASS; **law 3 (path) worst 5.568px on 0.38% of path-fight ticks** — the deepest pin transient recorded, inside the disclosed ≤6px/≤2% band with shrinking headroom, *recorded not judged*; 0 shot overlaps over 519,534 stone-bearing frames; 12,192 shots, 0 lost / 0 orphans / 0 double-departures. Entry: *Solidity suite — first run*. The 169-cell matrix stayed DEFERRED that day (owner decision — that entry is still live below).
- **The diagnostic probes retained from it**: `.qa/natscan.mjs` (per-seed natural-roll health) and `.qa/boomdiag.mjs` (forced-cell shot tracer). Entry: *Diagnosis record*.
- **The reveal tune that moved the sim timeline**: `revealTicks` 165 → **285**, land 0.40/0.58 → **0.32/0.46** (both still live); fingerprints re-baselined, the delta proof exact — every battle grew by precisely **120 ticks × rounds**, dynamics/rolls/outcomes untouched; `chromecheck`'s stub-frame advance went 150 → 250 for it. Entry: *Reveal +1s tune*.
- **Hitstop sanctioned at trigger policy 1**: 60ms per damage hit, **400ms on the killing blow** (`CFG.hitStopMs` / `koStopMs`, both still live) — frame-loop layer, no fingerprint movement. Entry: *Hitstop*.
- **The parry-loop fix, option 1**: `parryPush` 150 → 525 (later **700**, 2026-10-21), with the flutter gate as law 2's second sanctioned extension. Entry: *Parry-push tune*.
- **The K.O. kill still shipped** (owner pick "1", renderer-only) — and the K.O. *card* was removed two days later at owner word ("i dont want it to be announced"), leaving the freeze + the faded death-mark. Entry: *K.O. kill still*.
- **The inscribe fix — `CIRCLES_V2`**: every weapon's covering circles regenerated union-inscribed in the drawn ink — the permanent gate's doctrine (poke ≤ 0.12px, miss inside the disclosed band) and the origin of the seed-63230 K.O.-frame lesson: a circle poking past the ink fires hits across a **visible gap**. Entry: *The inscribe fix*.
- **The renderer+boot code-review pass**, fingerprints byte-identical and proven: it fixed the bow's arrow being the only shot whose hitbox disc was not inked (every shot now routes through `drawShot`; `HALO` gained `orb` 0.28 + `arrow` steel 0.18) and the K.O. still's never-drawn fallback (`consumeEvents` read `WEAPONS[id].kind`, which does not exist — kind lives on `stone` — so a shot fired AND landed inside one tick drew nothing). Its one "reported, not changed" item became the 2026-10-21 **REACH fix**, the first live entry below. Entry: *Code-review pass*.

**The 169-cell forced matrix is DEFERRED (owner decision, 2026-10-19).** Attempted the same day; the assistant's sandbox cannot host a run of that scale — background processes are silently reaped, foreground calls cap at 30 min, and the chunked retries destabilized the session (owner: "getting errors now, and thats exactly why i told you i dont want 1 hour"). Abandoned after part 0 by owner word; no matrix numbers exist for the 13-weapon roster. The matrix is the balance-pass instrument and the balance pass is deferred anyway, so nothing actionable is blocked. When the balance session wants it: one command **on the owner's machine** — `node measure.mjs` (full tier; ≈1,400 battles, wall ≈ 1h ÷ cores — minutes on a normal dev box; the report is proven identical at any `--jobs` count), or `--matrix-only` chunks on explicit request.

**Staff redesign replaced by the owner's hand-made pass on the review sheet (2026-10-24, owner: "try and replace our staff with this one").** The owner pasted a complete `INGR_STAFF` block of his own (pre-scale spine-local polys in the game's medium, vis flags doc-only) and it went into `scratch/redesign-preview.mjs` verbatim as `DESIGN.staff`, retiring my bezel-ring crown design from the sheet. The hand pass: gnarled tapered KW shaft d0-78 (one knot at d~30 on +l, three uneven grain dashes), two UNEQUAL KW prongs forking from the shaft top and cradling an irregular hand-cut KB gem (centre 88.5,0, r~8.5) with one KP glint, and the sigil = a cloth KC lashing binding the prong roots to the shaft (d 66-78) with a swallow-tailed end trailing to +l and two KD cord-turn lines on it — zero KG on the weapon, the lashing is the only ownership colour. Audit re-run, all 22 still pass: staff KC x1, maxD(pre) 100.0 (radial extent hypot(100,6) = 100.18 <= LT), tip still d 100 = len 100 so the orb's tip-launch point is unchanged, and the silhouette is still CHANGED so the staff stays in the 10-redesign ghost set (union delta vs canonical now 517px of 2338, was 112px for the bezel crown) — at landing its `CIRCLES_V2` row is deleted and rebaked per the runbook, and the owner's block is INGR-ready to land as-is. Sheet re-rendered (SVG + PNG, staff cell QA'd at zoom: prongs/gem/lashing/grain all read at played scale); canonical md5 asserted equal before/after the run and both frozen md5s re-verified after the GAMES.md edit — base freeze intact, nothing landed.

**Paste workflow adopted as the standing roster-redesign loop (2026-10-25, owner: "ok i prefer this approach going forward, i paste the code here for a specific weapon, you add it to the roster reedesign, and eventually you will add all to the game, do you think thats good?").** Answered yes — the staff pass had already proven the shape end to end — with four conditions attached, all now written into the tool header as a PASTE PROTOCOL block: (1) pasted INGR blocks go in **verbatim** under `DESIGN.<id>`, header comment kept, no translation or cleanup; (2) **scale is detected, not assumed** — the audit already divides art maxD by `S` (and by `KX` for the crossbow, the one authoring-convention trap), so a pre-scale block lands as-is, and if a paste matches the canonical len under neither reading it is called ambiguous and asked about rather than guessed; (3) **rule-breaks are reported with the measured number, never silently clamped** — KC x1, radial maxD <= LT = 113.44, the launch anchors (bow off 26, crossbow off 34, knives off 38, tome off 10, scroll off 11, staff/wand `fire:'tip'`, chain joint distances) — and the **mace stays the LT sentinel** whose 8-spike envelope must remain byte-identical or every `len: LT` row moves (crossbow's `KX = LT/116` included), so a mace paste is treated as a deliberate LT change and surfaced, not absorbed; (4) keepers may be pasted freely — if the block moves a keeper's drawn union the tool flags it and the row joins the rebake list, which is information rather than failure. Also recorded: landing is **one** delivery at the end (one row-splice rebake, one fingerprint ritual, one suite), not one per paste, so the base freeze stays up throughout the paste phase. A new living tally, `scratch/paste-ledger.md`, carries all 22 rows with measured len/hold/cls/maxD(pre)/union-delta, the art source per row, and the running delete-and-rebake list; at this run it reads 1 owner paste (staff) / 9 qwen-passed redesigns / 12 keepers, 10 rows invalidated and 12 proven valid. Tool re-run to confirm the header edit touched no behaviour: all 22 still pass (KC x1 each, keepers union pixel-identical, LT sentinel unmoved), sheet re-emitted byte-identical in size (SVG 239029 B, PNG 198197 B), canonical md5 asserted equal before/after (`e9662ebf9d038b5d974a5282fad59e8b`) and both frozen md5s re-verified after this edit (`spinclash-preview.html` thrash-r11, `roster-redesign-brief.md` `d51444b17303e1639da7c0c33c89de84`) — nothing landed. Open fork, asked and skipped: the artist session is redesigning the same 22 rows against the same frozen base, so its scope (sigils only / re-pass from the sheet / stand down) still needs an owner word before pastes and its delivery can both land.

**Claude artist session commissioned as the paste-loop's designer (2026-10-25, owner: "can you make a prompt i can paste to claude to design our weapons").** New deliverable `scratch/claude-design-prompt.md` — the prompt IS the file: the owner pastes it together with the canonical `spinclash-preview.html` into the Claude session, Claude acks in ≤3 lines and waits, the owner names ONE weapon per message, and every design comes back as a fixed 4-part delivery: ≤5-bullet note → self-contained preview `.html` artifact → the final code block → a measured audit line. The prompt encodes: the owner's distilled sigil doctrine VERBATIM (diegetic only; exactly one KC element; recolor-first; add-only-interior where none exists; never on KP; the scroll's KR ribbon and the tome's KP pages are not sigil material) with his amendment for this commission — "it is allowed to change geometry": a full redesign pass where a moved silhouette is legal but must be disclosed as the ghost tier (row deleted, rebake) vs row-valid (recolor/interior only); the house style ("smooth 2d … look like they came from the same artist and not ai" — flat fills, the renderer's 3px ink is the only linework, no gradients/texture/glow, legibility lock, hand-made asymmetry and material logic, the ~11.5u haft/shaft doctrine, 4–14 shapes, gold sparingly on fittings); and the hard gates as measured downstream — identity frozen (the INGR block is the ONLY output; len/hold/tuck/stone/lead/headLead/chain untouchable), pre-scale spine-local coordinates, the LT = 113.44 ceiling with hammer/axe/spear authored AT 113.4 so the boot normalization factor is 1 and the block means the same thing in tool and game, the crossbow authored on the 116 ceiling (KX = LT/116), the mace as LT sentinel (envelope maxD stays 113.44 or it is a deliberate LT change, surfaced never absorbed), the launch anchors (bow off 26, crossbow off 34, knives off 38, tome off 10, scroll off 11, staff tip d 100 / wand tip d 59), chain topology locked by `segOfShape` index (flail/nunchaku = recolor+interior only), hold frames and l-side conventions kept, `vis` paint strictly interior, and report-never-clamp on any collision.
A per-weapon identity table covers all 22 rows (const name — the four baked-JSON blocks HAMMER/DAGGER/BOW/AXE come back in authored `build([...])` form — len, hold, launch, owner-history notes; the owner-iterated looks are flagged as carrying playtest history). The preview spec reuses the game's OWN pipeline copied from the pasted source (palette consts, build/rect/star/arcBand/maxD, the boot scale, polyPath, drawWeapon, spineFrame, SPAN, bodyR 64, SPINE_R0 0.96, TH.botInk, COLS) and must show: new art large at true played scale with a carrier toggle (#a8892f / #ff6b4a / KG fallback), the old art beside it, in-fight context on a disc at 3–4 spin angles (both fp mirrors for lead/headLead weapons, static rest pose acceptable for chains), and an on-page `<pre>` audit measured by the artifact itself with `console.assert` gates — numbers measured, not remembered. **Loop effect:** Claude's blocks return through the standing paste protocol as owner pastes (ledger source column will read "claude pass"); the ingest format is the canonical `const INGR_<NAME> = build([...])` with header comment, so at the first such paste the tool gets a verbatim INGR adapter (its `mkS(shapes, S)` wrapper shares the identical shape-list core).
This also moves the open artist-session fork: the Claude session is now commissioned as the designer feeding the paste loop — `roster-redesign-brief.md` stays frozen and unused until an owner word formally retires or repurposes it. Both frozen md5s re-verified after this edit (`e9662ebf9d038b5d974a5282fad59e8b`, `d51444b17303e1639da7c0c33c89de84`); nothing landed, base freeze intact.

**Visuals-only arena test page built for judging the new weapon art in game (2026-10-25, owner halt + redirect: \"had to stop you, since you said 40+ minutes, i dont want you to build the harness etc, this is just for the visuals, i want alle the new weapons code put in ther by claude\").** The owner stopped the 40-50 minute locked arena-test plan and re-scoped it: no harness-building ceremony, just the looks, with the NEW weapons in — answering the pending set question in favour of the redesign set. Delivered `scratch/arena-test.html` (271984 B, generated — never hand-edited) via a new generator `scratch/arena-test.mjs`: it re-dumps the CURRENT design polys out of `scratch/redesign-preview.mjs` (new `--dump-polys` export mode → `scratch/design-polys.json`, so every future owner/Claude paste shows up on a one-command rebuild) and copies the frozen canonical file under a before+after md5 gate, applying exactly 16 loud line-anchored patches (each must match one line/range or the generator throws): the NEWPOLY art override injected AFTER the chain bootstrap + MINR/REACH/SPAN/POC loops (sim-facing geometry keeps canonical reads; hitboxes irrelevant — visuals only), bodyCollide/bladeVsBody/bladeVsBlade/stone-vs-fighter all gated off (no damage, no KOs, the round never ends), `cycleWeapon(G)` hot-swapping fighter 0 every 480 ticks in place (the round-roll reset minus the teleport: pose + velocity carried, spin direction kept at the new row's omega, fireAcc zeroed, `flailInit`, pop-in ramp replayed via phaseT=0, flipAnim snapped per the resetFx rule), forced open `['bow','mace']` so the cycle walks the roster in order (the hidden fighter 1 holds the LT sentinel and never fires), fighter 1 never drawn in the fight pass (still in the sim data — the loops are hardcoded to 2), the UNBAKED watermark replaced by a one-line weapon label, lastShots cleared per frame (one endless fight has no reveal to clean it), all DOM chrome hidden by CSS with every element left in place (the additive chrome script must not crash), resize()/framing copied untouched. At build time NO Claude delivery had landed yet — the page carries the current DESIGN set (9 qwen drafts + the owner's staff paste + 12 keepers); when Claude blocks arrive through the paste protocol, `node scratch/arena-test.mjs` picks them up with zero extra work. Proof (`scratch/arena-test-smoke.mjs`, the existing .qa fingerprint fakeEl boot pattern reused, not a new harness): 11000 headless ticks with `draw()` interleaved every 97 ticks — full 22-weapon cycle + wrap at the exact 480k cadence (bow→…→orb→bow), 20 shots thrown / max 3 in flight / hits-parries-swats-landed all 0, hp 100/100, phase fight, staff override verified in the booted WEAPONS (12 shapes, KC lashing at idx 4). Canonical md5 `e9662ebf9d038b5d974a5282fad59e8b` asserted before === after generation; base freeze intact, nothing landed.

**Orb round-out + the regen-splice lesson (2026-10-27, owner: "can you make the orb round, right now it has jagged edges"; `build orb-r14`, md5 `8984105b…`).** The magic orb body (14-gon), the staff head orb (hand-irregular 12-gon) and the flying staff-orb shot (16-gon — renderer-only; shot hitboxes are their r discs) were recut as regular 32-gons at identical centre and circumradius (nothing shrinks or moves; phase = vertex 0). Coverings re-baked by `.qa/bake-rows.mjs` (staff+orb: 2 baked, 20 keeper rows byte-identical). **Incident, caught before any gate ran:** the first covering paste used `inscribe --regen`'s WHOLE-block output, which silently re-baked four untouched weapons (crossbow, boomerang, flail, nunchaku — regen's whole-block chain/keeper bakes differ from the landing-era row-splice bakes); the keeper block was restored from the `scratch/arena-test.html` snapshot of the previous canonical and re-spliced with `bake-rows`, and the byte-identical forced cells below are the restoration proof. **Standing lesson: partial art edits splice via `bake-rows`; `--regen`'s whole block is not a splice source** (inscribe's header comment read otherwise at the time — **debt PAID 2026-10-28**: the header now carries the `!! WHOLE-BLOCK RE-DERIVATION ONLY — NOT A SPLICE SOURCE !!` warning with this incident named and the `bake-rows` pointer). Proofs: fingerprints vs baseline moved exactly one cell — natural s7, whose round 1 is the orb mirror (attributed by round-roll inspection with the new `.qa/rollattr.mjs`); every forced cell and natural s11 byte-identical; inscribe GATE PASS, chromecheck PASS, reveal-probe ALL PASS, projectile-fix PROBE PASS. Stamp `build orb-r14`.

**Gold retired from art + roundness class + orb band flush (2026-10-27, owner pick B on the sweep sheet, class extension "do this also", playtest: "it looks good with the new color"; `build sweep-r15`, md5 `abd707d7…`).** (1) The orb's sigil band ends flush on the body rim — its end vertices ride the body's own rim vertices/edges (38/70 at l0, edge-matched at ±2.6): zero air sliver, no poke past the silhouette, union ink unchanged. **Clinch spring — sustained interlocks are self-dissolving now (2026-10-27, owner pick 1 of the bind-prevention menu, after two reverted eyes-first passes: `parry-r16` (gate raise + recoil brake + rim-slide package) — "no it looks bad" — and `chain-r17` (quick-succession escalation ladder) — rejected at the eyes; both reverted byte-exact to sweep-r15; owner verdict on this build: "i think it fixes it, i dont see it anymore"; `build clinch-r18`, md5 `23a94fe1…`).** While the blade latch stays shut (blades inside the pad) a separation force ramps zero→full over `clinchRamp` 48 ticks (smoothstep onset, no jerk), pushes the two bodies apart along their own axis at up to `clinchAcc` 2200 px/s², and fades over `clinchDecay` 3/tick once the blades clear (~0.13 s): a force, never an impulse, active only while the sustained clash exists, exactly zero in open space — the locked blades are the visible cause of the push. The diagnosis that led here (`.qa/seedtrace.mjs`, seed 99797 at sweep-r15): the rim-pinned point-blank bind re-clanged at gate pace (min gap 17t, 15 gaps ≤30t, five burst windows) because nothing opposed *being inside* the interlock zone — the flutter gate capped the rhythm but the pair re-entered after every ejection; treating the exit (escalation) failed at the eyes, treating the persistence (the spring) worked. Confirmation at clinch-r18: 99797 runs min gap 57t, **zero gaps ≤30t, zero burst windows**, median gap 369t (open-fencing cadence); fingerprints moved on all eight cells (the force acts on every sustained clash; structurally zero whenever the latch is open — the dial block, two state lines, one helper and two loop lines are the whole change); inscribe GATE PASS, chromecheck PASS, reveal-probe ALL PASS, projectile-fix PROBE PASS with the informational termination red at 6/18 (was 4/18): separated pairs lengthen some cap-prone cells — termination is balance-pass property (open question 1), recorded not fixed; the clinch triple joins that question's knob list. Proofs: `.qa/fp-sweep-after.json` (pre-baseline) / `.qa/fp-clinch-after.json` (the new baseline).

**First sanctioned measure run since 2026-10-19 — quick tier, BOTH builds (2026-10-27, owner word "quick" after clinch-r18's eye-pass): the law-replica red set is PRE-CLINCH.** `measure --quick` on clinch-r18 and on a byte-exact sweep-r15 reconstruction (`.qa/baseline-sweep-r15.html`, md5 `abd707d7…`) yields the SAME red set: contact laws 1/2/4 and projectile laws 2s/3s FAIL on both builds (phantom hits ≈6% of hits — worst gap ~145-150px; parries-without-touch ≈7%; one-damage replica mismatches ≈490-520 with stuck-flag mismatches ≈58-60k; swats-without-touch 15-16; rendered stone-blade overlaps 74-80, worst ~27-35px), plus the three known deferred pace reds (termination line, 300s line, stall alarm). The clinch spring adds NO red and improves two pace numbers: worst stall 7.21s → 6.79s, longest battle 374.5s → 352.9s; laws 5, 6, 1s, 4s, 5s, 6s, 7s PASS on both. Attribution window: the last all-green law run was v1.2 (2026-10-19), so the drift landed with the post-v1.2 sim changes (chain-class per-segment bakes, roster-r12's 19-row rebake + knives volley + copy-kind spawns, orb-r14/sweep-r15 coverings) — deep attribution belongs to the balance & skill rework (open question 1), where the full matrix and the owner's machine live; recorded here with counts so the rework starts measured, not anecdotal. **[ATTRIBUTED 2026-10-28 — see the `chainlaw` entry below: ALL FIVE red channels are proven HARNESS chain-blindness (`measure.mjs` never passed the stored chain pose to `bladeCircles`/`parryGap`), and laws 1/2/2s/4/3s all clear to exactly ZERO once it does; the sim is honest on those channels. The unquoted 13th check (contact law 3) is also attributed: 575 of its 578 visible overlaps were chain fabrications, and the 3 that remain are rigid-only cells — one single-frame 10.09px katana+shuriken crossing that is NOT the disclosed pin class, so law 3 stays red for a genuinely new reason. The harness fix has LANDED (owner go); the re-proof run is all that is owed.]** Bind metrics on 99797 under clinch-r18 vs sweep-r15: min parry gap 57t (was 17t), gaps ≤30t zero (was 15), burst windows zero (was five), median gap 369t (was 177t) — the grind rhythm is gone, matching the owner's eyes ("i think it fixes it, i dont see it anymore").

**Carry-kill at the clang — recoils became legible (2026-10-27, owner k-pick 0.65 after his three-screenshot read of 99797: "i am 100% positive that axe was spinning the same way thow whole time"; verdict: "it looks good now"; `build carry-r19`, md5 `06fd78f8…`).** At the parry tick both bodies' existing velocity is scaled by (1 − `parryCarryKill` 0.65) BEFORE the shove is added: the clang absorbs the flight, and post-clang motion is shove + reversed orbit, so the rule-5 recoil reads on the head's screen path AT the clang instead of being masked by body carry. The diagnosis behind it: at 99797's t510 clang the axe's spin flipped per law (law-5 audit: 0 violations), but the head's screen path kept descending 14 more ticks under the shove-into-rim carry and then rode the rim-bounce carry upward for ~1 s — the sim truth held while the viewer contract ("what you see is what hits") bent at the perceptual level. Proofs: fingerprints 8/8 moved and every moved cell carries parries (8–109 per cell — the change is local to the parry trigger by construction), rebaselined at `.qa/fp-carry-after.json`; inscribe GATE PASS, chromecheck PASS, reveal-probe ALL PASS, projectile-fix PROBE PASS with the termination red back at 4/18 (was 6/18 under clinch-r18 — absorbed flights end cap-prone stalemates sooner); measure --quick red set IDENTICAL to the sweep-r15 baseline and clinch-r18 runs (laws 1/2/4/2s/3s remain the owed pre-clinch drift for the rework window; pace reds deferred) — no new drift; worst stall 6.96 s and longest battle 341.6 s, both slightly better than baseline. Scope: one dial, two lines at the trigger, stamp carry-r19.

### Retired from GAMES.md L361 (2026-10-28)

**The 2026-10-27 law red set ATTRIBUTED — it is harness chain-blindness, not a sim violation (2026-10-28, owner go "go" on the proposed probe; new read-only instrument `.qa/chainlaw.mjs`; canonical `63c5f113…` = pause-r20 + cleanup #3 + hygiene pass #1, untouched — the probe is read-only and no sim line moved).** The five red channels recorded on 2026-10-27 (laws 1/2/4 + 2s/3s, identical on clinch-r18 and the byte-exact sweep-r15 reconstruction) are reproduced exactly, and three of them clear to ZERO when the recompute is given the chain pose the sim already stores on the event. **Mechanism:** `bladeCircles(x, y, ang, wId, out, fl, fp)` branches on its 6th argument — `if (W.chain && fl) { chainFrames(…); …segment frames… }` else the rigid straight spine — so passing `fl = undefined` for flail/nunchaku SILENTLY places their covering circles along the rigid spine (near the haft) instead of on the articulated segment frames (out at the whipped ball). `measure.mjs` passes `undefined` at all four of its `bladeCircles` sites (lines 386/455/534/609) and builds both `parryGap` pose objects without an `fl` field (lines 338/397); it holds **zero** references to `aFl`, `bFl`, `fFl` or `auditFl`, and its own line-386 comment discloses the gap ("fl stays unparsed — the chain-pose law channel remains the disclosed gap"). The sim side is complete and says it is for the audit: hit events store `aFl` (canonical :1460, "the audit passes it to bladeCircles"), parry events `aFl`+`bFl` (:1648, "entry chain poses"), swat events `fFl` (:1765, "swatter chain pose"), and `G.auditFl` carries both fighters' poses per audited substep (:1201, stride 13). Every sim-side contact site passes the live pose — `bladeVsBody` :1403, the parry trigger :1529-30, the resolver :1569-70, the swat test :1827, the hitbox overlay :2222 — and canonical :1249 states the design ("Contact stays honest: the hit/parry/swat laws read the chain through bladeCircles(…, f.fl.u)"). `.qa/killstill.mjs` consumes `e.aFl` (chain-aware since the 2026-10-22 batch-4 fix) and is green; `.qa/leadproof.mjs` Part B is green because its cells are rigid-only (katana/scythe); `inscribe` gates art-vs-hitbox, a different axis; the fingerprint ritual proves determinism, never law compliance. **No probe that could have caught it was looking at a chain weapon through a geometry channel.** **Measured (20 naturals, 7 containing a chain weapon, + the solidity gauntlet's chain trio; 78s):**

| channel | RIGID (`fl` undefined — what `measure.mjs` does today) | CHAIN-AWARE (`fl` = the stored pose) | recorded 2026-10-27 |
|---|---|---|---|
| law 1 damage-on-touch | naturals **46/467 (9.85%)**, worst gap **149.14px**; forced **38/116 (32.76%)**, worst **145.47px** | **0/467** and **0/116** | phantom hits ≈6%, worst ~145-150px |
| law 2 parry exact touch | naturals **46/595 (7.73%)**; forced **33/82 (40.24%)** | **0/595** and **0/82** | parries-without-touch ≈7% |
| law 2s swat exact touch | naturals **7/207 (3.38%)**, worst 36.47px | **0/207** | swats-without-touch 15-16 |
| law 1s CONTROL (stone landings — chain-independent) | **0/293** | **0/293** | PASS |

Every rigid red lands on a chain-weapon event (46/46, 46/46, 7/7 — the attribution column equals the red column), the chain-aware column is zero across 583 natural + 198 forced events, the control channel is green in both passes (so the reimplementation is faithful), and the surgical check reports 99 naturals + 71 forced verdict flips with **zero non-chain verdicts moved**. The worst gaps are the same measurement the 2026-10-27 run saw (149.14 / 145.47px vs "~145-150px") and the law-2 rate matches (7.73% vs ≈7%). **Part D — the pose error itself**, sampled every tick (flail 2,959,712 circle placements, nunchaku 1,913,568): centre displacement **max 176.83px / mean 25.66px** (flail) and **max 169.32px / mean 27.37px** (nunchaku), radius delta 0.000px — same circles, wrong frame. `parryGap`'s reported `Infinity` is explained, not an artifact: its coarse box prune (`dx > rA+rB+parryPad`) rejects every pair when the rigid circles sit 57.7-99.2px off (measured on the two Infinity cases in seed 2's nunchaku mirror), whose chain-aware gaps are −0.234 and −0.420px — clean touches. **A second finding the same instrument exposed: the rigid fallback errs in BOTH directions.** Of 41 chain parries in that battle, 20 read as law-2 violations (15 Infinity + 5 finite positive) and **21 pass law 2 while showing FABRICATED overlaps down to −17.12px** — rigid circles interpenetrating where the truth is a −0.4px touch.
Fabricated overlap is law 3's territory, and the 2026-10-27 record enumerates only 12 of the 13 checks (it never says whether law 3 passed), so law 3 must be read in the same fix rather than assumed green. **What this changes:** laws 1, 2 and 2s are harness-only fixes — four `bladeCircles` arguments, two `parryGap` pose objects — with **no sim change, no fingerprint movement and no re-baseline**. Laws 4 and 3s are the two per-substep channels this probe deliberately does NOT reimplement (they need `auditFl` parsed and matched by substep index, and law 4 is a state machine); their mechanism is identical and their data is stored, but they stay unproven until the sanctioned run. **Owed:** the `measure.mjs` edit itself (4 + 2 sites, plus the `auditFl` parser for 4/3s) and one sanctioned run — which must not happen before the edit, or the same phantom reds re-report and get re-deferred. Any chain-aware residual from that run stays owed and must be reported separately, not folded in. **[FIX LANDED THE SAME DAY (owner decisions: fix `measure.mjs` now, no measure run; read law 3 in the same fix). The probe was then extended from three channels to ALL FIVE recorded red channels plus a proof of the new pose route, and re-run (20 naturals + 5 forced cells, 3m49s; output kept at `scratch/chainlaw-full-20261028.txt`, following the `scratch/gatecheck-partB.txt` precedent).]** **What landed in `uploads/measure.mjs` (harness-only — canonical `63c5f113…` untouched, fingerprints 8/8 byte-identical, chromecheck PASS, inscribe GATE PASS, `measure.mjs` NOT run per the standing owner-go rule; the probe validated the logic in its place):** the four `bladeCircles` calls now pass the pose the sim's own contact sites pass (`e.aFl` at the law-1 hit, `e.fFl` at the law-2s swat, `fl0`/`fl1` from `G.auditFl` in the law-4 replica, `entryFl[foeI]` in the law-3s stone pass); both `parryGap` pose objects carry `fl` (`gapAt` for laws 3/3-info, `e.aFl`/`e.bFl` for law 2); a new module-level `flAt(FL, sub, fi)` parses `G.auditFl` (stride 13: sub + 6 + 6) into reusable scratch and returns null on a missing row; and — the part the probe forced out — **`G.auditFl` has NO row for the `sub = -1` entry record**, which is exactly the record laws 3 and 3s read, so those take their pose from the live `fl.u` stashed immediately before `tick()`.
That stash is exact, not an approximation: `fl.u` is written only by `flailStep` (inside the substep loop) and `flailInit` (spawn and every round reset, to the rest constant `[1,0,1,0,1,0]`), so at the stash instant the live pose IS the pose the entry snapshot reads. Four coverage counters went in (`chainPoseTicks`, `chainPoseSubs`, `chainStoneEntries`, `flMissing`), wired through `ACC_ADD` + `accSnapshot` + the worker-merge destructuring so they survive `--jobs`, plus a new check line `[PASS/FAIL] harness chain-pose integrity` asserting `flMissing === 0` — a missing pose would silently degrade that one recompute back to the rigid spine. **Results, all five channels (RIGID = `measure.mjs` before the fix, AWARE = after; naturals 20 battles / forced 5):**

| channel | RIGID naturals | RIGID forced | CHAIN-AWARE | recorded 2026-10-27 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| law 1 damage-on-touch | 46/467 (9.85%), worst 149.14px | 62/164 (37.80%), worst 145.47px | **0 and 0** | phantom hits ≈6%, ~145-150px |
| law 2 parry exact touch | 46/595 (7.73%) | 49/131 (37.40%) | **0 and 0** | parries-without-touch ≈7% |
| law 2s swat exact touch | 7/207 (3.38%), worst 36.47px | 3/6, worst 44.18px | **0 and 0** | swats-without-touch 15-16 |
| law 4 one-damage replica | **248** replica + **28,549** stuck-flag | 252 + 22,373 | **0 + 0, PASS** | ≈490-520 replica, ≈58-60k flag |
| law 3s stone-vs-blade | 23 overlaps, worst 18.72px | 3, worst 6.87px | **0, PASS** | 74-80 overlaps, worst ~27-35px |
| law 3 rendered solidity | 846 overlaps, **578 visible**, worst **33.87px** → FAIL | 694 / 592 visible / 42.32px → FAIL | naturals 307 overlaps, **3 visible (0.001%)**, worst **10.09px** → still FAIL on the worst-depth clause alone; forced 191 / **0 visible** / 1.10px → **PASS** | never quoted (12 of 13 checks enumerated) |
| law 1s CONTROL | 0/293 | 0/7 | 0 and 0 | PASS |

So **four of the five recorded red channels clear to exactly zero** and law 3 loses 575 of its 578 visible overlaps. **Law 3's residual is NOT chain-related and is genuinely owed:** all three remaining visible cases sit in RIGID-only cells — `katana+shuriken` natural s5 tick 2690 at **10.092px**, `shield+javelin` s11 t20650 at 3.592px, `tome+katana` s20 t1059 at 3.361px (the latter two inside the disclosed band; only the worst-depth clause fails, and the visible-share clause passes by a factor of ~2000). A one-off diagnostic on s5 classified the 10.09px case: it lasts a **SINGLE rendered tick** with the bodies **149.51px apart** (2·bodyR = 128) — so it is NOT the disclosed infeasible-pin class, which requires bodies touching plus a buried wide head at ≤6px for ≤2 ticks. It is one frame of a blade crossing the resolver left deeper than the band's 6px line, previously invisible under the chain fabrications' 33.87px. **Part E — the new pose route, proven rather than assumed:** E1 compares the `sub = -1` record against the live pre-tick fighter state field by field (x, y, wAngle, bladeStuck, spin, alive × 2 fighters) on every fight tick:
**4,417,968 comparisons, 0 mismatches**, which is what licenses reading `fl.u` from the same instant. E2 bounds the stash's staleness by measuring it against substep 0's stored pose on the same body pose: max **2.98px** (naturals) / **3.13px** (forced), mean 0.10/0.09px — substep-scale, versus Part D's rigid error of up to 176.83px. `flMissing` 0 everywhere; surgical check 99 + 114 verdict flips, **zero non-chain verdicts moved**; Part D on the larger set: flail 3,769,864 circle placements (max 176.83px, mean 25.22px), nunchaku 2,259,552 (max 169.32px, mean 26.91px), radius delta 0.000px. **Two probe bugs were caught by the first smoke run and fixed before the record run — both would have produced confident nonsense:** law 4's rising-edge sets are PER-TICK scratch in `measure.mjs` (only stuck/latchT/peak are per-battle state), and leaking them across the battle reported 118,129 replica mismatches against the recorded ~500; and the forced chain trio contains no projectile weapon, so law 3s had **0 chain-foe frames** and its two columns could not differ — `flail+bow` and `nunchaku+crossbow` were added to the forced set and the report now prints an explicit VACUOUS warning whenever 3s has no chain-foe coverage.
**Now owed: only the sanctioned `measure.mjs` run** (owner's go, owner's machine) to re-prove all 13 checks; the expected post-fix state is 12 PASS with law 3 red on its worst-depth clause alone, plus the three known deferred pace reds. **[AMENDED 2026-10-28 — the run has now happened, and this expectation was right in its conclusion but unverifiable when written.]** Law 3 had been unconditionally red since 2026-10-21 because `polyOverlapVis` was missing from the worker snapshot, so the check evaluated `NaN <= 0.02` and no clause could be read at all. With that one identifier restored: the 3-seed quick tier gives **17 PASS / 1 FAIL** (law 3 green, only the deliberate stall red), while the 484-cell matrix does go red on law 3's worst-depth clause exactly as predicted here — band class 14.5-20.8px against the disclosed <6px, in all five landed slices including one with no chain weapon. See the measurement entry. **New rework item (open question 1): the katana+shuriken single-frame 10.09px crossing — either the band's 6px line moves with a recorded justification, or the resolver does. It is a sim/render question, not a harness one, and it must not be closed by re-calibrating the band silently.** **[ATTRIBUTED AND CLOSED THE SAME DAY (owner word: "fix it now"). That framing — "a sim/render question" — is SUPERSEDED: the residual is neither a resolver failure nor a render defect, and no sim line moved.]** **Mechanism, measured on all three cases.** Not shot knock (nothing moves a body position after `bladeVsBlade`: `stoneHitBody` touches velocity only, swats nothing, `fireStones` has no recoil), not a resolver stall (ΔwAngle across the tick boundary is exactly 0.00000 rad, so no nudge fired; the resolver's own push since the last snapshot was 0.17–0.53px), not chain-related.
It is the **edge-leading mirror**: a parry flips both spins at the END of `bladeVsBlade` (:1642-43) and `leadFlip(w, spin)` mirrors a flagged weapon's covering circles instantly while the bodies stay exactly where the resolver put them — so when the parry lands in the tick's LAST substep, no resolver pass ever sees the mirrored pose and the rendered frame inherits it. Recomputing the SAME body pose under the pre-flip mirror: katana+shuriken s5 t2690 **−10.092px → −0.149px**, shield+javelin s11 t20650 **−3.592px → −0.115px**, tome+katana s20 t1059 **−3.361px → −0.084px** — i.e. the pre-flip pose is a clean touch in every case, and both spins change sign across the tick (A +4.700 → −4.700, B −7.000 → +7.000 in the katana case; the parry fired at sub 16 of 16). The common factor is a FLAGGED weapon (katana `lead`, javelin `headLead`), not the projectile each cell happens to contain — that correlation was a red herring. **Why it is not a defect:** `CFG.flipMs` (canonical :129) already records the owner-accepted trade (decision 2026-10-22, raised 90 → 180ms at playtest: "its still too snappy") — the art's lateral coordinate animates old → edge-on → mirrored as `from·cos(π·p)` "while the sim's hitbox is already at its new (true) pose … the sim/laws/audits never see it (fingerprints byte-identical)".
Law 3 audits sim truth by specification, so it was correctly reporting a pose the sim genuinely holds for one substep. **Part F measured what the viewer draws rather than asserting it:** replicating the renderer's sweep (`from·cos(π·p)`, 180ms, sampled at each tick's entry pose across the window — 88 samples over the three cases) the worst DRAWN gap is **−0.149px at p = 0.00**, sub-ink. The drawn blade starts on the pre-flip side (a clean touch), collapses onto the spine at edge-on, and reaches the mirrored side ~22 ticks later, by which time the resolver has long separated the pair — the mirror frame lasts exactly ONE rendered tick in all three cases. **The rejected alternative:** re-resolving after the flip would teleport the bodies apart by up to 10px at the frame boundary — the same one-substep-teleport class the owner rejected on 2026-10-21 ("the old uncapped one-substep teleport read as a ~36px one-frame warp on long weapons"), it would make the DRAWN art jump (a visible defect replacing an invisible one), and it would move dynamics for every flagged weapon and re-baseline the fingerprints to fix a pose that is never drawn.
**What landed instead (harness-only, `measure.mjs`):** law 3 now splits every rendered overlap into the named MIRROR class and the BAND class. Membership is proven per occurrence, never inferred: (1) a parry fired at the previous tick's last substep — the very condition law 5's sign audit already carves out through `prevParrySubs` — with a `lead`/`headLead` weapon in play, and (2) the identical body pose under the pre-flip mirror clears the 3px ink (`gPre > −2.5px`). **The band's own calibration is untouched**: the gate is now `polyBandWorst < 6 && (polyOverlapVis − polyFlipVis)/polyTicks ≤ 2%`, so anything the mechanism does not explain stays in the band and still fails it. Added: a `gapAtFp` helper (explicit displayed factors), `polyFlipOverlap`/`polyFlipVis` in `ACC_ADD`, `polyFlipWorst`/`polyBandWorst` in `ACC_MAX`, the class split in law 3's metric line and its full disclosure in the check line. The class is deliberately NOT depth-gated — a shift bound would be vacuous, since the mirror moves the javelin's butt circles 144.5px, the scythe's 200.8px and the katana's 30.4px — so the class's COUNT is the regression signal, reported in full.
**Measured with the extended probe (20 naturals + 5 forced cells, 3m54s; output `scratch/chainlaw-full-20261028.txt`, which now carries Parts D/E/F):** mirror class **4 overlaps (3 visible), worst 10.092px, 0 UNEXPLAINED**; band class worst **1.738px**, visible **0/308,716** → **contact law 3 PASS**; forced cells mirror 0, band worst 1.100px → PASS; Part F worst drawn gap −0.149px. **Expected state at the next sanctioned run: 13 of 13 law checks PASS**, plus the three known deferred pace reds (termination, 300s, stall alarm) which belong to open question 1. Proofs: canonical `63c5f113…` untouched, fingerprints 8/8 byte-identical, chromecheck PASS, inscribe GATE PASS, every script parses.

**The facing pass — the dagger and the staff join the edge-leading mirror, build `lead-r22` (2026-10-28; owner playtest report verbatim: "i just noticed the dagger in game, it should have same behaviour as scythe and katana, if you look closely the dagger is not symmetrical, it has this white line which hints which way it is facing"; scope pick "dagger + staff together". The audit **reproduces every documented case**, which is the reason to trust it: sword/spear/hammer/axe carry KP in mirrored pairs (double bevels, correctly unflagged); the orb has zero off-axis circles and its KP is a specular highlight, matching the convention's "a sphere has no facing"; flail and nunchaku have no KP and are boot-forbidden the flags regardless; the javelin already carries `headLead`. Exactly two rows had a one-sided striking face and no flag — the dagger and the staff — and both are now `lead: -1`. All four flagged rows carry **−1**: every authored business contour in the roster sits on the −l side.
**Disclosed for the staff.** Most of its l-asymmetry is *not* a cutting face: it is the spiral grip wrapping (KD bands alternating sides at authored d 10.4…28.6, 20.8…35.1, 36.7…42.3, 52.0…75.4) plus a forked head mirrored to within 1.3 px — and all of that mirrors now along with the KP band, which is why this row carries the roster's largest mirror delta (22 off-axis circles, worst residual 21.02 px, outline 24.2 px), concentrated in the head region d 86…130 scaled = world radius 147…191 px, the striking zone. **The sign was an eye call and is now CONFIRMED at owner playtest, same day: "i confirm staff is looking good"** — so `lead: -1` stands for the staff as shipped, wrap and all, and no flip was needed (the scythe, by contrast, shipped at +1 and was flipped to −1 at playtest). Its tip launch point is on the spine (l 0) and `fireShots` does not read `leadFlip`, so `fire: 'tip'` is untouched by the mirror.
**Deliberately not proposed:** the mace is l-asymmetric without being directional (its eight KP flange highlights spiral — the +l set runs d 82→146, the −l set d 77→135, ~17 px offset — so mirroring would shuffle highlights rather than turn a blade), and the tangential rows' one-sided KP sits on the *radial* axis, where the doctrine's tool is `headLead`, not `lead`. Both are recorded so the next reader does not re-litigate them as misses.
